let customMasterRecords = [];
let customMasterName = 'imported-object';
let customMasterMaxSize = 1;
let customMasterFaceCount = 0;
let customMasterTopology = null;

function cloneCustomMaterial(material) {
  if (Array.isArray(material)) return material.map((m) => m?.clone ? m.clone() : m);
  return material?.clone ? material.clone() : new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xc6d4e8, roughness: 0.65, metalness: 0.02 });
}
function disposeCustomMaster() {
  for (const rec of customMasterRecords) {
    rec.geometry?.dispose?.();
    const material = rec.material;
    if (Array.isArray(material)) material.forEach((m) => m?.dispose?.()); else material?.dispose?.();
  }
  customMasterRecords = [];
  customMasterFaceCount = 0;
  customMasterTopology = null;
}
function topologyEdgeKey(a, b) { return a < b ? `${a}|${b}` : `${b}|${a}`; }
function triangleAreaSq(a, b, c) { return b.clone().sub(a).cross(c.clone().sub(a)).lengthSq() * 0.25; }
function signedVolumeOfTriangles(vertices, triangles) {
  let volume6 = 0;
  for (const t of triangles) {
    const a = vertices[t[0]], b = vertices[t[1]], c = vertices[t[2]];
    volume6 += a.dot(b.clone().cross(c));
  }
  return volume6 / 6;
}
function buildTopologyEdgeMap(triangles) {
  const edges = new Map();
  triangles.forEach((t, ti) => {
    for (let e = 0; e < 3; e++) {
      const a = t[e], b = t[(e + 1) % 3];
      const key = topologyEdgeKey(a, b);
      let arr = edges.get(key);
      if (!arr) { arr = []; edges.set(key, arr); }
      arr.push({ ti, a, b });
    }
  });
  return edges;
}
function unifyTriangleWinding(triangles) {
  const edges = buildTopologyEdgeMap(triangles);
  const neighbours = Array.from({ length: triangles.length }, () => []);
  for (const refs of edges.values()) {
    if (refs.length !== 2) continue;
    const r0 = refs[0], r1 = refs[1];
    const sameDirection = r0.a === r1.a && r0.b === r1.b;
    neighbours[r0.ti].push([r1.ti, sameDirection]);
    neighbours[r1.ti].push([r0.ti, sameDirection]);
  }
  const state = new Int8Array(triangles.length);
  for (let start = 0; start < triangles.length; start++) {
    if (state[start]) continue;
    state[start] = 1;
    const queue = [start];
    while (queue.length) {
      const ti = queue.pop();
      for (const [nj, sameDirection] of neighbours[ti]) {
        const wanted = sameDirection ? -state[ti] : state[ti];
        if (!state[nj]) { state[nj] = wanted; queue.push(nj); }
      }
    }
  }
  for (let i = 0; i < triangles.length; i++) if (state[i] < 0) [triangles[i][1], triangles[i][2]] = [triangles[i][2], triangles[i][1]];
}
function extractBoundaryLoops(triangles) {
  const edges = buildTopologyEdgeMap(triangles);
  const directed = [];
  for (const refs of edges.values()) if (refs.length === 1) directed.push({ a: refs[0].a, b: refs[0].b });
  const outgoing = new Map();
  const degree = new Map();
  const bump = (v) => degree.set(v, (degree.get(v) || 0) + 1);
  directed.forEach((e, i) => {
    let arr = outgoing.get(e.a); if (!arr) { arr = []; outgoing.set(e.a, arr); }
    arr.push(i); bump(e.a); bump(e.b);
  });
  if ([...degree.values()].some((d) => d !== 2)) return { loops: [], boundaryCount: directed.length, complex: directed.length > 0 };
  const used = new Uint8Array(directed.length);
  const loops = [];
  for (let seed = 0; seed < directed.length; seed++) {
    if (used[seed]) continue;
    const first = directed[seed];
    const loop = [first.a, first.b]; used[seed] = 1;
    let current = first.b, guard = 0;
    while (current !== first.a && guard++ < directed.length + 2) {
      const candidates = outgoing.get(current) || [];
      const nextIndex = candidates.find((idx) => !used[idx]);
      if (nextIndex == null) break;
      used[nextIndex] = 1; current = directed[nextIndex].b;
      if (current !== first.a) loop.push(current);
    }
    if (current === first.a && loop.length >= 3) loops.push(loop);
    else return { loops: [], boundaryCount: directed.length, complex: true };
  }
  return { loops, boundaryCount: directed.length, complex: false };
}
function projectLoop2D(loop, vertices) {
  const pts = loop.map((i) => vertices[i]);
  const normal = new THREE.Vector3();
  for (let i = 0; i < pts.length; i++) {
    const a = pts[i], b = pts[(i + 1) % pts.length];
    normal.x += (a.y - b.y) * (a.z + b.z);
    normal.y += (a.z - b.z) * (a.x + b.x);
    normal.z += (a.x - b.x) * (a.y + b.y);
  }
  const ax = Math.abs(normal.x), ay = Math.abs(normal.y), az = Math.abs(normal.z);
  if (ax >= ay && ax >= az) return pts.map((p) => new THREE.Vector2(p.y, p.z));
  if (ay >= ax && ay >= az) return pts.map((p) => new THREE.Vector2(p.x, p.z));
  return pts.map((p) => new THREE.Vector2(p.x, p.y));
}
function fillBoundaryLoops(vertices, triangles) {
  const result = extractBoundaryLoops(triangles);
  if (!result.boundaryCount) return { added: 0, loops: 0, complex: false };
  if (result.complex) return { added: 0, loops: 0, complex: true };
  let added = 0;
  for (const originalLoop of result.loops) {
    const loop = originalLoop.slice().reverse();
    const shape = projectLoop2D(loop, vertices);
    const tris2d = THREE.ShapeUtils.triangulateShape(shape, []);
    for (const tri of tris2d) {
      const t = [loop[tri[0]], loop[tri[1]], loop[tri[2]]];
      const a = vertices[t[0]], b = vertices[t[1]], c = vertices[t[2]];
      if (triangleAreaSq(a, b, c) > 1e-24) { triangles.push(t); added++; }
    }
  }
  return { added, loops: result.loops.length, complex: false };
}
function topologyStats(triangles) {
  const edges = buildTopologyEdgeMap(triangles);
  let boundary = 0, nonManifold = 0;
  for (const refs of edges.values()) {
    if (refs.length === 1) boundary++;
    else if (refs.length !== 2) nonManifold++;
  }
  return { edges: edges.size, boundary, nonManifold, manifold: boundary === 0 && nonManifold === 0 };
}
function repairCustomMasterGeometry(root) {
  root.updateMatrixWorld(true);
  const rawTriangles = [], rawPoints = [], sourceMaterials = [];
  root.traverse((obj) => {
    if (!obj.isMesh || !obj.geometry?.attributes?.position) return;
    const g = obj.geometry, pos = g.getAttribute('position'), index = g.index, base = rawPoints.length;
    for (let i = 0; i < pos.count; i++) rawPoints.push(new THREE.Vector3(pos.getX(i), pos.getY(i), pos.getZ(i)).applyMatrix4(obj.matrixWorld));
    const count = index ? index.count : pos.count;
    for (let i = 0; i + 2 < count; i += 3) rawTriangles.push([base + (index ? index.getX(i) : i), base + (index ? index.getX(i + 1) : i + 1), base + (index ? index.getX(i + 2) : i + 2)]);
    if (obj.material) sourceMaterials.push(obj.material);
  });
  if (!rawTriangles.length || !rawPoints.length) throw new Error('No usable triangle geometry was found in the master object.');
  const bounds = new THREE.Box3(); rawPoints.forEach((p) => bounds.expandByPoint(p));
  const diag = bounds.getSize(new THREE.Vector3()).length();
  const userTol = Number(customWeldTolerance.value || 0);
  const tolerance = userTol > 0 ? userTol : Math.max(diag * 1e-6, 1e-9);
  const inv = 1 / tolerance, grid = new Map(), vertices = [];
  const mapIndex = new Int32Array(rawPoints.length);
  for (let i = 0; i < rawPoints.length; i++) {
    const p = rawPoints[i], qx = Math.round(p.x * inv), qy = Math.round(p.y * inv), qz = Math.round(p.z * inv);
    let found = -1;
    for (let dx = -1; dx <= 1 && found < 0; dx++) for (let dy = -1; dy <= 1 && found < 0; dy++) for (let dz = -1; dz <= 1 && found < 0; dz++) {
      const list = grid.get(`${qx + dx},${qy + dy},${qz + dz}`); if (!list) continue;
      for (const vi of list) if (vertices[vi].distanceToSquared(p) <= tolerance * tolerance) { found = vi; break; }
    }
    if (found < 0) {
      found = vertices.length; vertices.push(p.clone());
      const key = `${qx},${qy},${qz}`; let list = grid.get(key); if (!list) { list = []; grid.set(key, list); } list.push(found);
    }
    mapIndex[i] = found;
  }
  const triangles = [], seen = new Set();
  let degenerateRemoved = 0, duplicateRemoved = 0;
  const areaEpsSq = Math.max(diag * diag * 1e-20, 1e-30);
  for (const rt of rawTriangles) {
    const t = [mapIndex[rt[0]], mapIndex[rt[1]], mapIndex[rt[2]]];
    if (t[0] === t[1] || t[1] === t[2] || t[2] === t[0] || triangleAreaSq(vertices[t[0]], vertices[t[1]], vertices[t[2]]) <= areaEpsSq) { degenerateRemoved++; continue; }
    const sorted = t.slice().sort((a,b) => a-b).join('|');
    if (seen.has(sorted)) { duplicateRemoved++; continue; }
    seen.add(sorted); triangles.push(t);
  }
  if (!triangles.length) throw new Error('Repair removed all triangles from the master object.');
  unifyTriangleWinding(triangles);
  let holesAdded = 0, holeLoops = 0, complexBoundary = false;
  if (customFillHoles.checked) {
    const fill = fillBoundaryLoops(vertices, triangles);
    holesAdded = fill.added; holeLoops = fill.loops; complexBoundary = fill.complex;
    if (holesAdded) unifyTriangleWinding(triangles);
  }
  if (signedVolumeOfTriangles(vertices, triangles) < 0) for (const t of triangles) [t[1], t[2]] = [t[2], t[1]];
  const stats = topologyStats(triangles);
  const positions = new Float32Array(vertices.length * 3);
  vertices.forEach((p, i) => { positions[i*3] = p.x; positions[i*3+1] = p.y; positions[i*3+2] = p.z; });
  const IndexArray = vertices.length > 65535 ? Uint32Array : Uint16Array;
  const indices = new IndexArray(triangles.length * 3);
  triangles.forEach((t, i) => { indices[i*3] = t[0]; indices[i*3+1] = t[1]; indices[i*3+2] = t[2]; });
  const geometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
  geometry.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(positions, 3));
  geometry.setIndex(new THREE.BufferAttribute(indices, 1));
  geometry.computeVertexNormals(); geometry.computeBoundingBox(); geometry.computeBoundingSphere();
  const material = sourceMaterials.length ? cloneCustomMaterial(sourceMaterials[0]) : new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xc6d4e8, roughness: 0.65, metalness: 0.02 });
  return { geometry, material, report: { rawVertices: rawPoints.length, weldedVertices: vertices.length, rawTriangles: rawTriangles.length, triangles: triangles.length, degenerateRemoved, duplicateRemoved, holesAdded, holeLoops, complexBoundary, tolerance, ...stats } };
}
function prepareCustomMaster(root, name) {
  disposeCustomMaster();
  let records = [], report = null;
  if (customRepairMaster.checked) {
    const repaired = repairCustomMasterGeometry(root);
    records = [{ geometry: repaired.geometry, material: repaired.material, name: 'RepairedMaster' }]; report = repaired.report;
  } else {
    root.updateMatrixWorld(true);
    root.traverse((obj) => {
      if (!obj.isMesh || !obj.geometry) return;
      const geometry = obj.geometry.clone(); geometry.applyMatrix4(obj.matrixWorld);
      if (!geometry.attributes.normal) geometry.computeVertexNormals();
      records.push({ geometry, material: cloneCustomMaterial(obj.material), name: obj.name || 'MasterPart' });
    });
    if (!records.length) throw new Error('No usable mesh geometry was found in the imported master object.');
    report = { manifold: false, boundary: -1, nonManifold: -1, tolerance: 0, unrepaired: true };
  }
  const bounds = new THREE.Box3();
  for (const rec of records) { rec.geometry.computeBoundingBox(); if (rec.geometry.boundingBox) bounds.union(rec.geometry.boundingBox); }
  if (bounds.isEmpty()) { records.forEach((r) => r.geometry.dispose()); throw new Error('Master object has empty bounds.'); }
  const centre = bounds.getCenter(new THREE.Vector3()), size = bounds.getSize(new THREE.Vector3());
  customMasterMaxSize = Math.max(size.x, size.y, size.z, 1e-9); customMasterFaceCount = 0;
  for (const rec of records) {
    rec.geometry.translate(-centre.x, -centre.y, -centre.z); rec.geometry.computeBoundingBox(); rec.geometry.computeBoundingSphere();
    customMasterFaceCount += rec.geometry.index ? rec.geometry.index.count / 3 : rec.geometry.getAttribute('position').count / 3;
  }
  customMasterRecords = records; customMasterName = name || 'imported-object'; customMasterTopology = report;
  if (report.unrepaired) customRepairStatus.textContent = '⚠ Master repair disabled — topology is not certified welded / watertight / manifold.';
  else if (report.manifold) customRepairStatus.textContent = `✓ MANIFOLD / WATERTIGHT • ${report.weldedVertices.toLocaleString()} welded vertices • ${report.triangles.toLocaleString()} triangles • 0 boundary edges • 0 non-manifold edges • ${report.holeLoops.toLocaleString()} boundary loop${report.holeLoops === 1 ? '' : 's'} repaired`;
  else customRepairStatus.textContent = `✕ REPAIR INCOMPLETE • ${report.boundary.toLocaleString()} boundary edges • ${report.nonManifold.toLocaleString()} non-manifold edges${report.complexBoundary ? ' • complex/open boundary could not be safely capped' : ''}`;
  customMasterStatus.textContent = `${customMasterName} • ${records.length.toLocaleString()} repaired master mesh${records.length === 1 ? '' : 'es'} • ${Math.round(customMasterFaceCount).toLocaleString()} faces • max size ${customMasterMaxSize.toPrecision(4)}${report.tolerance ? ` • weld tol ${report.tolerance.toPrecision(3)}` : ''}`;
  if (customStrictManifold.checked && !report.manifold) {
    disposeCustomMaster();
    throw new Error('Strict manifold validation failed. Master was rejected; adjust weld tolerance or repair the source mesh before instancing.');
  }
}
async function loadCustomMasterFile(file) {
  customMasterStatus.textContent = `Loading and repairing ${file.name}…`; customRepairStatus.textContent = 'Repair status: analysing topology…';
  const ext = file.name.split('.').pop()?.toLowerCase(); let loaded;
  if (ext === 'obj') loaded = new OBJLoader().parse(await file.text());
  else {
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(file);
    try {
      if (ext === 'glb' || ext === 'gltf') loaded = (await new GLTFLoader().loadAsync(url)).scene;
      else if (ext === 'stl') loaded = new THREE.Mesh(await new STLLoader().loadAsync(url), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xc6d4e8, roughness: 0.65, metalness: 0.02 }));
      else throw new Error('Unsupported master-object format.');
    } finally { URL.revokeObjectURL(url); }
  }
  prepareCustomMaster(loaded, file.name.replace(/\.[^.]+$/, '') || 'imported-object');
}
function updateCustomInstanceUI() {
  customInstanceSection.classList.toggle('hidden', replaceMode.value !== 'customInstance');
  const constant = customSizeMode.value === 'constant'; customRelativeControls.classList.toggle('hidden', constant); customConstantControls.classList.toggle('hidden', !constant);
}
replaceMode.addEventListener('change', updateCustomInstanceUI);
customSizeMode.addEventListener('change', updateCustomInstanceUI);
customRelativeScale.addEventListener('input', () => customRelativeScaleOut.value = `${Number(customRelativeScale.value).toFixed(2)}×`);
customTiltX.addEventListener('input', () => customTiltXOut.value = `${customTiltX.value}°`);
customTiltY.addEventListener('input', () => customTiltYOut.value = `${customTiltY.value}°`);
customInstanceFile.addEventListener('change', () => {
  const file = customInstanceFile.files?.[0];
  if (file) loadCustomMasterFile(file).catch((e) => { console.error(e); customMasterStatus.textContent = `Master load failed: ${e.message || e}`; if (!customMasterRecords.length) customRepairStatus.textContent = '✕ Master rejected'; });
  customInstanceFile.value = '';
});
function customAxisVector() {
  switch (customAxis.value) {
    case 'px': return new THREE.Vector3(1,0,0); case 'py': return new THREE.Vector3(0,1,0); case 'nx': return new THREE.Vector3(-1,0,0); case 'ny': return new THREE.Vector3(0,-1,0); case 'nz': return new THREE.Vector3(0,0,-1); default: return new THREE.Vector3(0,0,1);
  }
}
function customAxisCorrectionQuaternion() { return new THREE.Quaternion().setFromUnitVectors(customAxisVector(), new THREE.Vector3(0,0,1)); }
function customPlacementMatrixForFace(face) {
  if (!face || face.length < 3) return null;
  const centre = face.reduce((acc, p) => acc.add(p), new THREE.Vector3()).multiplyScalar(1 / face.length), normal = polygonNormal(face); if (!normal) return null;
  let inRadius = Infinity; for (let i = 0; i < face.length; i++) inRadius = Math.min(inRadius, pointSegmentDistance3D(centre, face[i], face[(i + 1) % face.length]));
  if (!Number.isFinite(inRadius) || inRadius <= 1e-9) return null;
  return customPlacementMatrix(torusLocalFrame(face, normal, centre), centre, inRadius * 2);
}
function customPlacementMatrixForNode(node) { if (!node?.normal || !node?.point) return null; return customPlacementMatrix(torusNodeFrame(node), node.point, Math.max((node.localRadius || 0) * 2, 1e-9)); }
function customPlacementMatrix(frame, point, localDiameter) {
  const basis = new THREE.Matrix4().makeBasis(frame.xAxis, frame.yAxis, frame.normal), qFrame = new THREE.Quaternion().setFromRotationMatrix(basis);
  const qTiltX = new THREE.Quaternion().setFromAxisAngle(new THREE.Vector3(1,0,0), THREE.MathUtils.degToRad(Number(customTiltX.value || 0)));
  const qTiltY = new THREE.Quaternion().setFromAxisAngle(new THREE.Vector3(0,1,0), THREE.MathUtils.degToRad(Number(customTiltY.value || 0)));
  const quaternion = qFrame.clone().multiply(qTiltY.clone().multiply(qTiltX)).multiply(customAxisCorrectionQuaternion());
  const wantedSize = customSizeMode.value === 'constant' ? Number(customConstantSize.value) : localDiameter * Number(customRelativeScale.value || 1);
  if (!Number.isFinite(wantedSize) || wantedSize <= 0 || !Number.isFinite(customMasterMaxSize) || customMasterMaxSize <= 0) return null;
  const uniformScale = wantedSize / customMasterMaxSize, position = point.clone().addScaledVector(frame.normal, Number(customNormalOffset.value || 0));
  return new THREE.Matrix4().compose(position, quaternion, new THREE.Vector3(uniformScale, uniformScale, uniformScale));
}
function customOrientedFaces(faces) { const previous = torusNormalMode.value; torusNormalMode.value = customNormalMode.value; try { return orientTorusFaces(faces); } finally { torusNormalMode.value = previous; } }
function cloneCustomGenerationRecords() { return customMasterRecords.map((rec) => ({ geometry: rec.geometry.clone(), material: cloneCustomMaterial(rec.material), name: rec.name })); }
async function generateCustomInstanceResult() {
  if (!sourceRoot) return;
  if (!customMasterRecords.length) { setStatus('Import a valid manifold master object before generating instances'); return; }
  if (customStrictManifold.checked && !customMasterTopology?.manifold) { setStatus('Master is not certified manifold — generation blocked'); return; }
  generateBtn.disabled = true;
  try {
    const facesResult = collectFaces(), oriented = customOrientedFaces(facesResult.faces), symmetryResult = applySymmetryToFaces(oriented), faces = symmetryResult.faces;
    const placementMode = customPlacementMode.value === 'node' ? 'node' : 'face', placements = placementMode === 'node' ? buildSharedNodePlacements(faces) : faces;
    if (!placements.length) { setStatus('No usable placement locations were found'); return; }
    const meshNodeCount = placements.length * customMasterRecords.length;
    if (meshNodeCount > 80000 && !confirm(`This will create ${meshNodeCount.toLocaleString()} instanced mesh nodes. Continue?`)) { setStatus('Generation cancelled'); return; }
    setStatus(`Creating ${placements.length.toLocaleString()} imported-object instances…`); await new Promise((resolve) => requestAnimationFrame(resolve));
    disposeObject(resultRoot); resultRoot = new THREE.Group(); resultRoot.name = 'ImportedMasterInstances';
    const generationRecords = cloneCustomGenerationRecords(), matrices = []; let skipped = 0;
    for (const placement of placements) { const matrix = placementMode === 'node' ? customPlacementMatrixForNode(placement) : customPlacementMatrixForFace(placement); if (matrix) matrices.push(matrix); else skipped++; }
    if (!matrices.length) { generationRecords.forEach((r) => r.geometry.dispose()); setStatus('No valid instance transforms could be generated'); return; }
    for (const rec of generationRecords) {
      const instanced = new THREE.InstancedMesh(rec.geometry, rec.material, matrices.length); instanced.name = rec.name || customMasterName; instanced.instanceMatrix.setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage);
      matrices.forEach((m, i) => instanced.setMatrixAt(i, m)); instanced.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true; resultRoot.add(instanced);
    }
    resultRoot.userData.customInstanceSet = { version: CUSTOM_WIP_VERSION, masterName: customMasterName, placementMode, count: matrices.length, manifold: !!customMasterTopology?.manifold };
    scene.add(resultRoot); sourceRoot.visible = showSource.checked; exportBtn.disabled = false;
    const label = placementMode === 'node' ? 'shared face nodes' : 'face centres';
    setStatus(`Generated ${matrices.length.toLocaleString()} instances of ${customMasterName} at ${label} • master ${customMasterTopology?.manifold ? 'manifold/watertight' : 'unverified'}${skipped ? ` • ${skipped.toLocaleString()} skipped` : ''}`);
    setOutputMetrics({ faces: Math.round(customMasterFaceCount * matrices.length), holes: matrices.length, exported: false });
  } catch (error) { console.error(error); setStatus(`Generation failed: ${error.message || error}`); } finally { generateBtn.disabled = false; }
}
function buildCustomSharedNodeExport() {
  const exportScene = new THREE.Scene(); exportScene.name = 'NomadImportedMasterInstances'; const matrix = new THREE.Matrix4();
  resultRoot.children.filter((c) => c.isInstancedMesh).forEach((instanced) => {
    for (let i = 0; i < instanced.count; i++) {
      instanced.getMatrixAt(i, matrix); const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(instanced.geometry, instanced.material); mesh.name = instanced.name || customMasterName; mesh.matrixAutoUpdate = false; mesh.matrix.copy(matrix); mesh.userData = nomadNodeExtras(matrix, false); exportScene.add(mesh);
    }
  });
  exportScene.userData = { meshLineReplacer: { version: CUSTOM_WIP_VERSION, instanceMode: 'shared-gltf-nodes', master: customMasterName, target: 'Nomad Sculpt', manifoldMaster: !!customMasterTopology?.manifold } }; return exportScene;
}
function buildCustomBakedExport() {
  const root = new THREE.Group(); root.name = 'BakedImportedMasterCopies'; const matrix = new THREE.Matrix4();
  resultRoot.children.filter((c) => c.isInstancedMesh).forEach((instanced) => {
    for (let i = 0; i < instanced.count; i++) { instanced.getMatrixAt(i, matrix); const geometry = instanced.geometry.clone(); geometry.applyMatrix4(matrix); root.add(new THREE.Mesh(geometry, cloneCustomMaterial(instanced.material))); }
  }); return root;
}
function removeSharedExportRoot(root) { if (!root) return; while (root.children.length) root.remove(root.children[root.children.length - 1]); }
function exportCustomInstanceGlb() {
  if (!resultRoot) return;
  if (customStrictManifold.checked && !customMasterTopology?.manifold) { setStatus('Export blocked — master is not certified manifold'); return; }
  exportBtn.disabled = true; const shared = customExportMode.value === 'instances'; setStatus(shared ? 'Building Nomad shared-node imported-object GLB…' : 'Baking imported-object copies…');
  const exportRoot = shared ? buildCustomSharedNodeExport() : buildCustomBakedExport();
  new GLTFExporter().parse(exportRoot, (result) => {
    const blob = new Blob([result], { type: 'model/gltf-binary' }), url = URL.createObjectURL(blob), a = document.createElement('a'), placement = customPlacementMode.value === 'node' ? 'node' : 'face';
    a.href = url; a.download = `${sourceName}-${customMasterName}-${placement}-${shared ? 'instances' : 'baked'}.glb`; document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); a.remove(); setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 3000);
    if (shared) removeSharedExportRoot(exportRoot); else disposeObject(exportRoot);
    setStatus(shared ? 'Nomad shared-node imported-object GLB exported' : 'Baked imported-object GLB exported'); setOutputMetrics({ exported: true }); exportBtn.disabled = false;
  }, (error) => {
    console.error(error); if (shared) removeSharedExportRoot(exportRoot); else disposeObject(exportRoot); setStatus(`Export failed: ${error.message || error}`); exportBtn.disabled = false;
  }, { binary: true, onlyVisible: true });
}
generateBtn.addEventListener('click', (event) => { if (replaceMode.value !== 'customInstance') return; event.preventDefault(); event.stopImmediatePropagation(); generateCustomInstanceResult(); }, true);
exportBtn.addEventListener('click', (event) => { if (replaceMode.value !== 'customInstance') return; event.preventDefault(); event.stopImmediatePropagation(); exportCustomInstanceGlb(); }, true);
updateCustomInstanceUI();

  if (opt) opt.textContent = 'Grouped Nomad instances';
}
if (typeof customExportMode !== 'undefined' && customExportMode?.options?.length) {
  const opt = [...customExportMode.options].find((o) => o.value === 'instances');
  if (opt) opt.textContent = 'Grouped Nomad instances';
}

function groupedNomadExtras(matrix, selected = false) {
  return nomadNodeExtras(matrix, selected);
}

buildNomadSharedNodeScene = function(instancedMesh) {
  const exportScene = new THREE.Scene();
  exportScene.name = 'NomadGroupedTorusInstances';

  const group = new THREE.Group();
  group.name = 'Generated Instances';
  group.matrixAutoUpdate = false;
  group.matrix.identity();
  group.userData = groupedNomadExtras(group.matrix, false);
  exportScene.add(group);

  const sharedGeometry = instancedMesh.geometry;
  const sharedMaterial = instancedMesh.material;
  const matrix = new THREE.Matrix4();
  let made = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < instancedMesh.count; i++) {
    instancedMesh.getMatrixAt(i, matrix);
    const e = matrix.elements;
    const sx = Math.hypot(e[0], e[1], e[2]);
    const sy = Math.hypot(e[4], e[5], e[6]);
    const sz = Math.hypot(e[8], e[9], e[10]);
    if (sx < 1e-10 || sy < 1e-10 || sz < 1e-10) continue;

    const node = new THREE.Mesh(sharedGeometry, sharedMaterial);
    node.name = `Torus ${String(made + 1).padStart(3, '0')}`;
    node.matrixAutoUpdate = false;
    node.matrix.copy(matrix);
    node.userData = groupedNomadExtras(matrix, false);
    group.add(node);
    made++;
  }

  exportScene.userData = {
    meshLineReplacer: {
      version: GROUPED_WIP_VERSION,
      instanceMode: 'shared-gltf-nodes',
      hierarchy: 'grouped',
      groupName: group.name,
      instanceCount: made,
      target: 'Nomad Sculpt'
    }
  };
  return exportScene;
};

if (typeof buildCustomSharedNodeExport !== 'undefined') {
  buildCustomSharedNodeExport = function() {
    const exportScene = new THREE.Scene();
    exportScene.name = 'NomadGroupedImportedMasterInstances';

    const group = new THREE.Group();
    group.name = `${customMasterName || 'Imported Master'} Instances`;
    group.matrixAutoUpdate = false;
    group.matrix.identity();
    group.userData = groupedNomadExtras(group.matrix, false);
    exportScene.add(group);

    const matrix = new THREE.Matrix4();
    let childCount = 0;
    const instancedParts = resultRoot.children.filter((c) => c.isInstancedMesh);

    instancedParts.forEach((instanced, partIndex) => {
      for (let i = 0; i < instanced.count; i++) {
        instanced.getMatrixAt(i, matrix);
        const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(instanced.geometry, instanced.material);
        const baseName = instanced.name || customMasterName || `Master Part ${partIndex + 1}`;
        mesh.name = `${baseName} ${String(i + 1).padStart(3, '0')}`;
        mesh.matrixAutoUpdate = false;
        mesh.matrix.copy(matrix);
        mesh.userData = groupedNomadExtras(matrix, false);
        group.add(mesh);
        childCount++;
      }
    });

    exportScene.userData = {
      meshLineReplacer: {
        version: GROUPED_WIP_VERSION,
        instanceMode: 'shared-gltf-nodes',
        hierarchy: 'grouped',
        groupName: group.name,
        master: customMasterName,
        placementCount: resultRoot.userData?.customInstanceSet?.count || 0,
        meshNodeCount: childCount,
        target: 'Nomad Sculpt'
      }
    };
    return exportScene;
  };
}

  }

const displaySection = showSource?.closest('.section');
const viewSection = document.createElement('div');
viewSection.className = 'section';
viewSection.id = 'diagnosticViewSection';
viewSection.innerHTML = `
  <h2>View mode</h2>
  <div class="control"><label for="viewTarget">View target</label><select id="viewTarget"><option value="result" selected>Generated result</option><option value="source">Source model</option><option value="both">Both</option></select></div>
  <div class="control"><label for="baseViewMode">Base view</label><select id="baseViewMode"><option value="solid" selected>Solid</option><option value="solidWire">Solid + Wire</option><option value="wire">Wireframe</option><option value="faceGroups">Face Groups</option><option value="normalOrientation">Normal Orientation</option><option value="analysis">Mesh Analysis</option></select></div>
  <label class="check"><input id="overlayWire" type="checkbox" /> Wireframe overlay</label>

  <label class="check"><input id="overlayVertexNormals" type="checkbox" /> Vertex normal arrows</label>
  <div class="control"><label for="normalLength">Normal length</label><output id="normalLengthOut">2.5%</output><input id="normalLength" type="range" min="0.5" max="12" value="2.5" step="0.5" /></div>
  <p class="note">Display-only diagnostics. Export geometry and materials remain unchanged. Mesh Analysis highlights boundary edges red and non-manifold edges magenta.</p>

const viewTarget = $('#viewTarget');
const baseViewMode = $('#baseViewMode');
const overlayWire = $('#overlayWire');
const overlayFaceNormals = $('#overlayFaceNormals');
const overlayVertexNormals = $('#overlayVertexNormals');
const normalLength = $('#normalLength');
const normalLengthOut = $('#normalLengthOut');

const viewHelpers = new THREE.Group();
viewHelpers.name = 'ViewportDiagnostics';
scene.add(viewHelpers);
const originalViewState = new WeakMap();
const VIEW_MAX_TRIANGLES = 40000;
const VIEW_MAX_NORMALS = 16000;
const VIEW_MAX_INSTANCE_HELPERS = 1500;

function disposeViewMaterial(material) {
  if (Array.isArray(material)) material.forEach((m) => m?.dispose?.());
  else material?.dispose?.();
}
function disposeViewHelperObject(obj) {
  obj.traverse?.((child) => {
    child.geometry?.dispose?.();
    disposeViewMaterial(child.material);
  });
  obj.removeFromParent?.();
}
function clearViewHelpers() {
  while (viewHelpers.children.length) disposeViewHelperObject(viewHelpers.children[viewHelpers.children.length - 1]);
}
function rememberViewState(mesh) {
  if (!originalViewState.has(mesh)) originalViewState.set(mesh, { material: mesh.material, geometry: mesh.geometry });
}
function restoreViewState() {
  const roots = [sourceRoot, resultRoot].filter(Boolean);
  for (const root of roots) root.traverse((obj) => {
    if (!obj.isMesh && !obj.isInstancedMesh) return;
    const state = originalViewState.get(obj);
    if (!state) return;
    if (obj.material !== state.material) disposeViewMaterial(obj.material);
    if (obj.geometry !== state.geometry) obj.geometry?.dispose?.();
    obj.material = state.material;
    obj.geometry = state.geometry;
  });
  clearViewHelpers();
}
function viewRoots() {
  if (viewTarget.value === 'source') return sourceRoot ? [sourceRoot] : [];
  if (viewTarget.value === 'both') return [sourceRoot, resultRoot].filter(Boolean);
  return resultRoot ? [resultRoot] : (sourceRoot ? [sourceRoot] : []);
}
function geometryDiagonal(geometry) {
  geometry.computeBoundingBox?.();
  if (!geometry.boundingBox || geometry.boundingBox.isEmpty()) return 1;
  return Math.max(geometry.boundingBox.getSize(new THREE.Vector3()).length(), 1e-6);
}
function triangleList(geometry) {
  const p = geometry?.getAttribute?.('position');
  if (!p) return [];
  const idx = geometry.index;
  const count = idx ? idx.count : p.count;
  const out = [];
  for (let i = 0; i + 2 < count && out.length < VIEW_MAX_TRIANGLES; i += 3) {
    out.push([idx ? idx.getX(i) : i, idx ? idx.getX(i + 1) : i + 1, idx ? idx.getX(i + 2) : i + 2]);
  }
  return out;
}
function localVertexKey(p, i, tolerance) {
  return `${Math.round(p.getX(i)/tolerance)},${Math.round(p.getY(i)/tolerance)},${Math.round(p.getZ(i)/tolerance)}`;
}
function buildTopologyDiagnostic(geometry) {
  const p = geometry?.getAttribute?.('position');
  if (!p) return { boundary: null, nonManifold: null, boundaryCount: 0, nonManifoldCount: 0 };
  const tris = triangleList(geometry);
  const tol = Math.max(geometryDiagonal(geometry) * 1e-7, 1e-9);
  const edgeMap = new Map();
  const add = (ia, ib) => {
    const ka = localVertexKey(p, ia, tol), kb = localVertexKey(p, ib, tol);
    const key = ka < kb ? `${ka}|${kb}` : `${kb}|${ka}`;
    let rec = edgeMap.get(key);
    if (!rec) { rec = { count: 0, ia, ib }; edgeMap.set(key, rec); }
    rec.count++;
  };
  for (const t of tris) { add(t[0],t[1]); add(t[1],t[2]); add(t[2],t[0]); }
  const boundaryPos = [], nonPos = [];
  let boundaryCount = 0, nonManifoldCount = 0;
  for (const rec of edgeMap.values()) {
    const target = rec.count === 1 ? boundaryPos : rec.count > 2 ? nonPos : null;
    if (!target) continue;
    if (rec.count === 1) boundaryCount++; else nonManifoldCount++;
    target.push(p.getX(rec.ia),p.getY(rec.ia),p.getZ(rec.ia),p.getX(rec.ib),p.getY(rec.ib),p.getZ(rec.ib));
  }
  const make = (arr, color) => {
    if (!arr.length) return null;
    const g = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
    g.setAttribute('position', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(arr,3));
    return new THREE.LineSegments(g, new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color, depthTest: false, transparent: true, opacity: 0.98 }));
  };
  return { boundary: make(boundaryPos, 0xff3838), nonManifold: make(nonPos, 0xff2dce), boundaryCount, nonManifoldCount };
}
function makeWireHelper(mesh) {
  const state = originalViewState.get(mesh) || { geometry: mesh.geometry };
  const edgeGeo = new THREE.WireframeGeometry(state.geometry);
  const mat = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color: 0x16191f, transparent: true, opacity: 0.72 });
  const lines = new THREE.LineSegments(edgeGeo, mat);
  lines.renderOrder = 12;
  return lines;
}
function copyMeshTransformToHelper(mesh, helper, matrixOverride = null) {
  helper.matrixAutoUpdate = false;
  if (matrixOverride) helper.matrix.multiplyMatrices(mesh.matrixWorld, matrixOverride);
  else helper.matrix.copy(mesh.matrixWorld);
}
function addHelperForMeshInstances(mesh, makeHelper) {
  if (!mesh.isInstancedMesh) {
    const helper = makeHelper(); if (!helper) return;
    copyMeshTransformToHelper(mesh, helper); viewHelpers.add(helper); return;
  }
  const matrix = new THREE.Matrix4();
  const count = Math.min(mesh.count, VIEW_MAX_INSTANCE_HELPERS);
  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    mesh.getMatrixAt(i, matrix);
    const helper = makeHelper(); if (!helper) continue;
    copyMeshTransformToHelper(mesh, helper, matrix); viewHelpers.add(helper);
  }
}
function makeNormalLines(mesh, vertexMode) {
  const state = originalViewState.get(mesh) || { geometry: mesh.geometry };
  const g = state.geometry;
  const p = g?.getAttribute?.('position');
  if (!p) return null;
  if (!g.getAttribute('normal')) g.computeVertexNormals();
  const n = g.getAttribute('normal');
  const len = geometryDiagonal(g) * (Number(normalLength.value) / 100);
  const pos = [], col = [];
  if (vertexMode) {
    const step = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(p.count / VIEW_MAX_NORMALS));
    for (let i = 0; i < p.count; i += step) {
      const a = new THREE.Vector3(p.getX(i),p.getY(i),p.getZ(i));
      const b = a.clone().add(new THREE.Vector3(n.getX(i),n.getY(i),n.getZ(i)).normalize().multiplyScalar(len));
      pos.push(a.x,a.y,a.z,b.x,b.y,b.z); col.push(0.2,0.75,1,0.2,0.75,1);
    }
  } else {
    const tris = triangleList(g);
    const step = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(tris.length / VIEW_MAX_NORMALS));
    for (let ti = 0; ti < tris.length; ti += step) {
      const t = tris[ti];
      const a = new THREE.Vector3(p.getX(t[0]),p.getY(t[0]),p.getZ(t[0]));
      const b = new THREE.Vector3(p.getX(t[1]),p.getY(t[1]),p.getZ(t[1]));
      const c = new THREE.Vector3(p.getX(t[2]),p.getY(t[2]),p.getZ(t[2]));
      const centre = a.clone().add(b).add(c).multiplyScalar(1/3);
      const normal = b.clone().sub(a).cross(c.clone().sub(a)).normalize();
      const end = centre.clone().addScaledVector(normal,len);
      pos.push(centre.x,centre.y,centre.z,end.x,end.y,end.z); col.push(0.3,1,0.45,0.3,1,0.45);
    }
  }
  if (!pos.length) return null;
  const geo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
  geo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(pos,3));
  geo.setAttribute('color', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(col,3));
  return new THREE.LineSegments(geo, new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ vertexColors:true, depthTest:false, transparent:true, opacity:0.9 }));
}
function makeFaceGroupGeometry(geometry) {
  const p = geometry?.getAttribute?.('position'); if (!p) return null;
  const source = geometry.toNonIndexed();
  const sp = source.getAttribute('position');
  const triCount = Math.floor(sp.count / 3);
  const colors = new Float32Array(sp.count * 3);
  const palette = [
    [0.94,0.35,0.32],[0.24,0.66,0.96],[0.35,0.82,0.46],[0.95,0.72,0.25],
    [0.68,0.43,0.94],[0.20,0.82,0.78],[0.95,0.48,0.72],[0.64,0.72,0.28]
  ];
  // Diagnostic face groups: geometry groups/material ranges when present; otherwise coplanar-normal families.
  const groupForTriangle = new Int32Array(triCount); groupForTriangle.fill(-1);
  if (geometry.groups?.length && geometry.index) {
    for (let gi=0; gi<geometry.groups.length; gi++) {
      const gr=geometry.groups[gi];
      const start=Math.floor(gr.start/3), end=Math.min(triCount,Math.ceil((gr.start+gr.count)/3));
      for(let t=start;t<end;t++) groupForTriangle[t]=gi;
    }
  }
  for (let t=0;t<triCount;t++) {
    let group=groupForTriangle[t];
    if (group<0) {
      const i=t*3;
      const a=new THREE.Vector3(sp.getX(i),sp.getY(i),sp.getZ(i));
      const b=new THREE.Vector3(sp.getX(i+1),sp.getY(i+1),sp.getZ(i+1));
      const c=new THREE.Vector3(sp.getX(i+2),sp.getY(i+2),sp.getZ(i+2));
      const normal=b.clone().sub(a).cross(c.clone().sub(a)).normalize();
      const ax=Math.abs(normal.x), ay=Math.abs(normal.y), az=Math.abs(normal.z);
      group = ax>=ay&&ax>=az ? (normal.x>=0?0:1) : ay>=ax&&ay>=az ? (normal.y>=0?2:3) : (normal.z>=0?4:5);
    }
    const color=palette[group%palette.length];
    for(let k=0;k<3;k++){const j=(t*3+k)*3;colors[j]=color[0];colors[j+1]=color[1];colors[j+2]=color[2];}
  }
  source.setAttribute('color',new THREE.BufferAttribute(colors,3));
  return source;
}
function makeNormalOrientationGeometry(geometry) {
  const source = geometry.toNonIndexed();
  const p=source.getAttribute('position'); if(!p)return source;
  const colors=new Float32Array(p.count*3);
  source.computeBoundingBox();
  const centre=source.boundingBox?.getCenter(new THREE.Vector3())||new THREE.Vector3();
  for(let i=0;i+2<p.count;i+=3){
    const a=new THREE.Vector3(p.getX(i),p.getY(i),p.getZ(i));
    const b=new THREE.Vector3(p.getX(i+1),p.getY(i+1),p.getZ(i+1));
    const c=new THREE.Vector3(p.getX(i+2),p.getY(i+2),p.getZ(i+2));
    const centroid=a.clone().add(b).add(c).multiplyScalar(1/3);
    const normal=b.clone().sub(a).cross(c.clone().sub(a)).normalize();
    const outward=normal.dot(centroid.clone().sub(centre))>=0;
    const color=outward?[0.22,0.9,0.36]:[0.96,0.22,0.22];
    for(let k=0;k<3;k++){const j=(i+k)*3;colors[j]=color[0];colors[j+1]=color[1];colors[j+2]=color[2];}
  }
  source.setAttribute('color',new THREE.BufferAttribute(colors,3));
  return source;
}
function applyBaseViewToMesh(mesh, mode) {
  rememberViewState(mesh);
  const state=originalViewState.get(mesh);
  if(mode==='solid') return;
  if(mode==='wire') {
    mesh.material=new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0xd7dce7,wireframe:true,transparent:true,opacity:0.95});
    return;
  }
  if(mode==='faceGroups') {
    mesh.geometry=makeFaceGroupGeometry(state.geometry)||state.geometry;
    mesh.material=new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({vertexColors:true,roughness:0.82,metalness:0,side:THREE.DoubleSide,flatShading:true});
    return;
  }
  if(mode==='normalOrientation') {
    mesh.geometry=makeNormalOrientationGeometry(state.geometry)||state.geometry;
    mesh.material=new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({vertexColors:true,roughness:0.8,metalness:0,side:THREE.DoubleSide,flatShading:true});
    return;
  }
  if(mode==='analysis') {
    mesh.material=new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({color:0x9ca6b5,roughness:0.85,metalness:0,side:THREE.DoubleSide,transparent:true,opacity:0.72});
  }
}
function applyViewMode() {
  restoreViewState();
  const roots=viewRoots();
  const mode=baseViewMode.value;
  let boundaryTotal=0, nonManifoldTotal=0;
  for(const root of roots){
    root.updateMatrixWorld(true);
    root.traverse((mesh)=>{
      if(!mesh.isMesh&&!mesh.isInstancedMesh)return;
      applyBaseViewToMesh(mesh,mode);
      const addWire = mode==='solidWire' || overlayWire.checked;
      if(addWire) addHelperForMeshInstances(mesh,()=>makeWireHelper(mesh));
      if(overlayFaceNormals.checked) addHelperForMeshInstances(mesh,()=>makeNormalLines(mesh,false));
      if(overlayVertexNormals.checked) addHelperForMeshInstances(mesh,()=>makeNormalLines(mesh,true));
      if(mode==='analysis') {
        const diagnostic=buildTopologyDiagnostic((originalViewState.get(mesh)||{}).geometry||mesh.geometry);
        boundaryTotal+=diagnostic.boundaryCount; nonManifoldTotal+=diagnostic.nonManifoldCount;
        if(diagnostic.boundary) addHelperForMeshInstances(mesh,()=>diagnostic.boundary.clone());
        if(diagnostic.nonManifold) addHelperForMeshInstances(mesh,()=>diagnostic.nonManifold.clone());
        diagnostic.boundary?.geometry?.dispose?.(); diagnostic.boundary?.material?.dispose?.();
        diagnostic.nonManifold?.geometry?.dispose?.(); diagnostic.nonManifold?.material?.dispose?.();
      }
    });
  }
  if(mode==='analysis') setStatus(`Mesh Analysis • ${boundaryTotal.toLocaleString()} boundary edges • ${nonManifoldTotal.toLocaleString()} non-manifold edges`);
}

[viewTarget,baseViewMode,overlayWire,overlayFaceNormals,overlayVertexNormals].forEach((el)=>el.addEventListener('change',applyViewMode));
normalLength.addEventListener('input',()=>{normalLengthOut.value=`${Number(normalLength.value).toFixed(1)}%`;if(overlayFaceNormals.checked||overlayVertexNormals.checked)applyViewMode();});
showSource.addEventListener('change',()=>setTimeout(applyViewMode,0));
generateBtn.addEventListener('click',()=>setTimeout(applyViewMode,80));
fileInput.addEventListener('change',()=>setTimeout(applyViewMode,250));
exampleBtn.addEventListener('click',()=>setTimeout(applyViewMode,80));
replaceMode.addEventListener('change',()=>setTimeout(applyViewMode,0));

// Keep display state coherent after async generation/import completes without touching the animation loop.
let lastViewSource=null,lastViewResult=null;
setInterval(()=>{
  if(sourceRoot!==lastViewSource||resultRoot!==lastViewResult){lastViewSource=sourceRoot;lastViewResult=resultRoot;applyViewMode();}
},500);

const DENSITY_WIP_VERSION = '0.8.9-wip.1';
document.title = `Mesh Line Replacer v${DENSITY_WIP_VERSION}`;
const densityVersionEl = document.querySelector('.version');
if (densityVersionEl) densityVersionEl.textContent = `v${DENSITY_WIP_VERSION}`;
const densityAboutVersion = document.querySelector('.about-version');
if (densityAboutVersion) densityAboutVersion.textContent = `Version ${DENSITY_WIP_VERSION}`;
const densityAppTitle = document.querySelector('.title');
if (densityAppTitle) densityAppTitle.textContent = 'Mesh Line Replacer · WIP';
const densityAboutBody = document.querySelector('.about-body');
if (densityAboutBody) {
  const lists = densityAboutBody.querySelectorAll('ul');
  if (lists.length > 1 && !densityAboutBody.textContent.includes('v0.8.9-wip.1')) {
    const li = document.createElement('li');
    li.innerHTML = '<strong>v0.8.9-wip.1</strong> — Five-step topology-aware instance density control';
    lists[1].prepend(li);
  }
}

function addDensitySlider(afterControl, id) {
  if (!afterControl || document.getElementById(id)) return document.getElementById(id);
  const wrap = document.createElement('div');
  wrap.className = 'control';
  wrap.innerHTML = `<label for="${id}">Instance density</label><output id="${id}Out">0</output><input id="${id}" type="range" min="-2" max="2" value="0" step="1" />`;
  afterControl.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', wrap);
  const input = wrap.querySelector('input');
  const output = wrap.querySelector('output');
  const update = () => { const v = Number(input.value); output.value = v > 0 ? `+${v}` : `${v}`; };
  input.addEventListener('input', update); update();
  return input;
}

const torusDensity = addDensitySlider($('#torusPlacementMode')?.closest('.control'), 'torusDensity');
const customDensity = addDensitySlider(typeof customPlacementMode !== 'undefined' ? customPlacementMode?.closest('.control') : null, 'customDensity');

function densityFaceCentre(face) {
  const p = new THREE.Vector3();
  face.forEach((v) => p.add(v));
  return p.multiplyScalar(1 / Math.max(face.length, 1));
}
function densityFaceNode(face) {
  if (!face || face.length < 3) return null;
  const point = densityFaceCentre(face);
  const normal = polygonNormal(face);
  if (!normal) return null;
  let minEdge = Infinity, tangent = null;
  for (let i = 0; i < face.length; i++) {
    const edge = face[(i + 1) % face.length].clone().sub(face[i]);
    const len = edge.length();
    if (len > 1e-9) {
      minEdge = Math.min(minEdge, len);
      if (!tangent || edge.lengthSq() > tangent.lengthSq()) tangent = edge;
    }
  }
  let inRadius = Infinity;
  for (let i = 0; i < face.length; i++) inRadius = Math.min(inRadius, pointSegmentDistance3D(point, face[i], face[(i + 1) % face.length]));
  return { point, normal: normal.clone(), tangent: tangent || new THREE.Vector3(1,0,0), localRadius: Number.isFinite(inRadius) && inRadius > 1e-9 ? inRadius : (Number.isFinite(minEdge) ? minEdge * 0.5 : 1), faceCount: 1 };
}
function densityEdgeNodes(faces) {
  const map = new Map();
  for (const face of faces) {
    const normal = polygonNormal(face);
    if (!normal) continue;
    const area = Math.max(faceAreaForNodeNormal(face), 1e-12);
    for (let i = 0; i < face.length; i++) {
      const a = face[i], b = face[(i + 1) % face.length];
      const ka = vertexKey(a), kb = vertexKey(b);
      const key = ka < kb ? `${ka}|${kb}` : `${kb}|${ka}`;
      let rec = map.get(key);
      if (!rec) {
        const edge = b.clone().sub(a);
        rec = { a: a.clone(), b: b.clone(), tangent: edge, length: edge.length(), normalSum: new THREE.Vector3(), faces: 0 };
        map.set(key, rec);
      }
      rec.normalSum.addScaledVector(normal, area); rec.faces++;
    }
  }
  const out = [];
  for (const rec of map.values()) {
    if (rec.length <= 1e-9 || rec.normalSum.lengthSq() < 1e-14) continue;
    out.push({
      point: rec.a.clone().add(rec.b).multiplyScalar(0.5),
      normal: rec.normalSum.normalize(),
      tangent: rec.tangent.clone(),
      localRadius: rec.length * 0.5,
      faceCount: rec.faces
    });
  }
  return out;
}
function densityRecordPoint(rec) { return rec.kind === 'face' ? densityFaceCentre(rec.source) : rec.source.point; }
function densityRecordsForFaces(faces, placementMode, level) {
  const base = placementMode === 'node'
    ? buildSharedNodePlacements(faces).map((p) => ({ kind: 'node', source: p }))
    : faces.map((f) => ({ kind: 'face', source: f }));
  if (level < 0) return spatialThinDensity(base, level === -1 ? 0.5 : 0.25);
  if (level === 0) return base;

  const records = base.slice();
  densityEdgeNodes(faces).forEach((p) => records.push({ kind: 'node', source: p }));
  if (level >= 2) {
    if (placementMode === 'node') faces.forEach((f) => records.push({ kind: 'face', source: f }));
    else buildSharedNodePlacements(faces).forEach((p) => records.push({ kind: 'node', source: p }));
  }
  return dedupeDensityRecords(records);
}
function dedupeDensityRecords(records) {
  if (!records.length) return records;
  const box = new THREE.Box3(); records.forEach((r) => box.expandByPoint(densityRecordPoint(r)));
  const diag = Math.max(box.getSize(new THREE.Vector3()).length(), 1e-8);
  const tol = diag * 1e-8;
  const seen = new Set(), out = [];
  for (const rec of records) {
    const p = densityRecordPoint(rec);
    const key = `${Math.round(p.x/tol)},${Math.round(p.y/tol)},${Math.round(p.z/tol)}`;
    if (seen.has(key)) continue;
    seen.add(key); out.push(rec);
  }
  return out;
}
function spatialThinDensity(records, ratio) {
  if (records.length < 3 || ratio >= 0.999) return records.slice();
  const target = Math.max(1, Math.round(records.length * ratio));
  if (target >= records.length) return records.slice();
  const points = records.map(densityRecordPoint);
  const box = new THREE.Box3(); points.forEach((p) => box.expandByPoint(p));
  const min = box.min.clone();
  const diag = Math.max(box.getSize(new THREE.Vector3()).length(), 1e-9);
  function choose(cell) {
    const cells = new Map();
    for (let i = 0; i < records.length; i++) {
      const p = points[i];
      const key = `${Math.floor((p.x-min.x)/cell)},${Math.floor((p.y-min.y)/cell)},${Math.floor((p.z-min.z)/cell)}`;
      if (!cells.has(key)) cells.set(key, i);
    }
    return [...cells.entries()].sort((a,b)=>a[0].localeCompare(b[0])).map(([,i])=>records[i]);
  }
  let lo = diag / 1e6, hi = diag * 2, best = records.slice(), bestErr = Math.abs(records.length-target);
  for (let iter=0; iter<20; iter++) {
    const mid=(lo+hi)*0.5, chosen=choose(mid), err=Math.abs(chosen.length-target);
    if(err<bestErr){best=chosen;bestErr=err;}
    if(chosen.length>target) lo=mid; else hi=mid;
  }
  if(best.length>target) best=best.slice(0,target);
  return best;
}
function densityTorusData(rec) { return rec.kind === 'face' ? torusInstanceDataForFace(rec.source) : torusInstanceDataForNode(rec.source); }
function densityCustomMatrix(rec) { return rec.kind === 'face' ? customPlacementMatrixForFace(rec.source) : customPlacementMatrixForNode(rec.source); }
function densityLabel(level) { return level > 0 ? `+${level}` : `${level}`; }

generateFaceTorusResult = async function() {
  const facesResult = collectFaces();
  const orientedFaces = orientTorusFaces(facesResult.faces);
  const symmetryResult = applySymmetryToFaces(orientedFaces);
  const faces = symmetryResult.faces;
  if (!faces.length) { setStatus('No usable source faces were found for torus replacement'); return; }
  const placementMode = $('#torusPlacementMode')?.value === 'node' ? 'node' : 'face';
  const level = Number(torusDensity?.value || 0);
  const records = densityRecordsForFaces(faces, placementMode, level);
  if (!records.length) { setStatus('No usable torus placements were found'); return; }
  if (records.length > 100000 && !confirm(`This density will create ${records.length.toLocaleString()} torus instances. Continue?`)) { setStatus('Generation cancelled'); return; }
  const valid = [];
  for (const rec of records) { const data = densityTorusData(rec); if (data) valid.push({ rec, data }); }
  if (!valid.length) { setStatus('No valid torus placements could be generated'); return; }
  setStatus(`Creating ${valid.length.toLocaleString()} torus instances • density ${densityLabel(level)}…`);
  await new Promise((resolve) => requestAnimationFrame(resolve));
  disposeObject(resultRoot);
  resultRoot = new THREE.Group();
  resultRoot.name = placementMode === 'node' ? 'NodeTorusInstanceSet' : 'FaceTorusInstanceSet';
  const first = valid[0].data;
  const masterGeometry = new THREE.TorusGeometry(first.masterMajor, first.masterTube, Number(torusTubeSegments.value), Number(torusRingSegments.value));
  masterGeometry.name = 'MasterTorusGeometry';
  const material = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xd7a6f4, roughness: 0.55, metalness: 0.06, flatShading: torusFlatShading.checked });
  material.name = 'MasterTorusMaterial';
  const instanced = new THREE.InstancedMesh(masterGeometry, material, valid.length);
  instanced.name = placementMode === 'node' ? 'SharedNodeTorusInstances' : 'FaceTorusInstances';
  instanced.instanceMatrix.setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage);
  valid.forEach(({data},i)=>instanced.setMatrixAt(i,data.matrix));
  instanced.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;
  resultRoot.add(instanced); scene.add(resultRoot);
  sourceRoot.visible = showSource.checked; exportBtn.disabled = false;
  const masterFaces = masterGeometry.index ? masterGeometry.index.count/3 : masterGeometry.getAttribute('position').count/3;
  const label = placementMode === 'node' ? 'shared face nodes' : 'face centres';
  setStatus(`Generated ${valid.length.toLocaleString()} shared torus instances • ${label} • density ${densityLabel(level)}${level>0?' • topology-derived additions':''}${level<0?' • spatially thinned':''}${symmetryResult.mirrored?` • mirrored across ${symmetryAxis.value.toUpperCase()} centre plane`:''}`);
  setOutputMetrics({ faces: Math.round(masterFaces*valid.length), holes: valid.length, exported:false });
};

if (typeof generateCustomInstanceResult !== 'undefined') {
  generateCustomInstanceResult = async function() {
    if (!sourceRoot) return;
    if (!customMasterRecords.length) { setStatus('Import a master object before generating instances'); return; }
    generateBtn.disabled = true;
    try {
      const facesResult = collectFaces();
      const oriented = customOrientedFaces(facesResult.faces);
      const symmetryResult = applySymmetryToFaces(oriented);
      const faces = symmetryResult.faces;
      const placementMode = customPlacementMode.value === 'node' ? 'node' : 'face';
      const level = Number(customDensity?.value || 0);
      const records = densityRecordsForFaces(faces, placementMode, level);
      if (!records.length) { setStatus('No usable placement locations were found'); return; }
      const matrices = [];
      for (const rec of records) { const matrix = densityCustomMatrix(rec); if (matrix) matrices.push(matrix); }
      if (!matrices.length) { setStatus('No valid instance transforms could be generated'); return; }
      const meshNodeCount = matrices.length * customMasterRecords.length;
      if (meshNodeCount > 80000 && !confirm(`This density will create ${meshNodeCount.toLocaleString()} instanced mesh nodes. Continue?`)) { setStatus('Generation cancelled'); return; }
      setStatus(`Creating ${matrices.length.toLocaleString()} imported-object instances • density ${densityLabel(level)}…`);
      await new Promise((resolve)=>requestAnimationFrame(resolve));
      disposeObject(resultRoot);
      resultRoot = new THREE.Group(); resultRoot.name='ImportedMasterInstances';
      const generationRecords = cloneCustomGenerationRecords();
      for (const rec of generationRecords) {
        const instanced = new THREE.InstancedMesh(rec.geometry, rec.material, matrices.length);
        instanced.name = rec.name || customMasterName;
        instanced.instanceMatrix.setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage);
        matrices.forEach((m,i)=>instanced.setMatrixAt(i,m));
        instanced.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate=true; resultRoot.add(instanced);
      }
      resultRoot.userData.customInstanceSet={version:DENSITY_WIP_VERSION,masterName:customMasterName,placementMode,count:matrices.length,density:level};
      scene.add(resultRoot); sourceRoot.visible=showSource.checked; exportBtn.disabled=false;
      const totalFaces=customMasterFaceCount*matrices.length;
      const label=placementMode==='node'?'shared face nodes':'face centres';
      setStatus(`Generated ${matrices.length.toLocaleString()} instances of ${customMasterName} • ${label} • density ${densityLabel(level)}${level>0?' • topology-derived additions':''}${level<0?' • spatially thinned':''}`);
      setOutputMetrics({faces:Math.round(totalFaces),holes:matrices.length,exported:false});
    } catch(error) { console.error(error); setStatus(`Generation failed: ${error.message||error}`); }
    finally { generateBtn.disabled=false; }
  };
}



// v0.8.9-wip.2 — expand instance density from -5 to +5
(function(){
  const VERSION='0.8.9-wip.2';
  document.title=`Mesh Line Replacer v${VERSION}`;
  const v=document.querySelector('.version'); if(v)v.textContent=`v${VERSION}`;
  const av=document.querySelector('.about-version'); if(av)av.textContent=`Version ${VERSION}`;
  for(const input of [document.getElementById('torusDensity'),document.getElementById('customDensity')]){
    if(!input)continue; input.min='-5'; input.max='5'; input.step='1';
    const out=document.getElementById(input.id+'Out');
    const update=()=>{const n=Number(input.value); if(out)out.value=n>0?`+${n}`:`${n}`;};
    input.addEventListener('input',update); update();
  }
  function edgeNodesV2(faces,subdivisions){
    const map=new Map();
    for(const face of faces){
      const normal=polygonNormal(face); if(!normal)continue;
      const area=Math.max(faceAreaForNodeNormal(face),1e-12);
      for(let i=0;i<face.length;i++){
        const a=face[i],b=face[(i+1)%face.length],ka=vertexKey(a),kb=vertexKey(b),key=ka<kb?`${ka}|${kb}`:`${kb}|${ka}`;
        let rec=map.get(key);
        if(!rec){const edge=b.clone().sub(a);rec={a:a.clone(),b:b.clone(),tangent:edge,length:edge.length(),normalSum:new THREE.Vector3(),faces:0};map.set(key,rec);}
        rec.normalSum.addScaledVector(normal,area); rec.faces++;
      }
    }
    const out=[];
    for(const rec of map.values()){
      if(rec.length<=1e-9||rec.normalSum.lengthSq()<1e-14)continue;
      const normal=rec.normalSum.clone().normalize();
      for(let s=1;s<=subdivisions;s++){
        const t=s/(subdivisions+1);
        out.push({point:rec.a.clone().lerp(rec.b,t),normal:normal.clone(),tangent:rec.tangent.clone(),localRadius:rec.length/(subdivisions+1)*0.5,faceCount:rec.faces});
      }
    }
    return out;
  }
  densityRecordsForFaces=function(faces,placementMode,level){
    const base=placementMode==='node'?buildSharedNodePlacements(faces).map(p=>({kind:'node',source:p})):faces.map(f=>({kind:'face',source:f}));
    if(level<0){const ratios={'-1':0.8,'-2':0.6,'-3':0.4,'-4':0.25,'-5':0.125};return spatialThinDensity(base,ratios[String(level)]||0.5);}
    if(level===0)return base;
    const records=base.slice();
    edgeNodesV2(faces,Math.max(1,level)).forEach(p=>records.push({kind:'node',source:p}));
    if(level>=2){
      if(placementMode==='node')faces.forEach(f=>records.push({kind:'face',source:f}));
      else buildSharedNodePlacements(faces).forEach(p=>records.push({kind:'node',source:p}));
    }
    return dedupeDensityRecords(records);
  };
  if(typeof DENSITY_WIP_VERSION!=='undefined'){
    const body=document.querySelector('.about-body');
    const lists=body?.querySelectorAll('ul');
    if(lists?.length>1&&!body.textContent.includes('v0.8.9-wip.2')){
      const li=document.createElement('li');li.innerHTML='<strong>v0.8.9-wip.2</strong> — Instance density expanded to ten divisions (−5 to +5)';lists[1].prepend(li);
    }
  }
})();

// v0.9.0-wip.3 — panel organisation / persistent status UI
(function(){
const VERSION='0.9.0-wip.3';document.title=`Mesh Line Replacer v${VERSION}`;const v=document.querySelector('.version');if(v)v.textContent=`v${VERSION}`;const av=document.querySelector('.about-version');if(av)av.textContent=`Version ${VERSION}`;

// v0.9.0-wip.5 — functional UI consolidation / de-duplication
(function(){
const VERSION='0.9.0-wip.5';
document.title=`Mesh Line Replacer v${VERSION}`;
const vv=document.querySelector('.version');if(vv)vv.textContent=`v${VERSION}`;
const av=document.querySelector('.about-version');if(av)av.textContent=`Version ${VERSION}`;
const style=document.createElement('style');style.textContent=`.mlr-consolidated-hidden{display:none!important}.mlr-filemenu .mlr-about-row{margin-top:0}.mlr-instance-name{font-size:12px;color:#aeb7c5;padding:5px 2px 2px}.mlr-empty-section{display:none!important}`;document.head.appendChild(style);

// FILE: explicit source and instance-object routing.
const filePop=document.querySelector('.mlr-filepop');
if(filePop){
  const sourceBtn=filePop.querySelector('button[data-act="source"]');
  const masterBtn=filePop.querySelector('button[data-act="master"]');
  if(sourceBtn){sourceBtn.textContent='Open Source Mesh…';sourceBtn.addEventListener('click',(e)=>{const input=document.getElementById('fileInput')||document.querySelector('input[type=file]:not(#customInstanceFile):not(#mlrProjectPicker)');if(input){e.stopImmediatePropagation();input.click();}},true)}
  if(masterBtn){masterBtn.textContent='Import Instance Object…';masterBtn.addEventListener('click',(e)=>{const input=document.getElementById('customInstanceFile');if(input){e.stopImmediatePropagation();input.click();}else alert('Instance-object importer is not available in the current mode.');},true)}
  const exportBtn=filePop.querySelector('button[data-act="export"]');if(exportBtn)exportBtn.textContent='Export GLB…';
  const menu=filePop.querySelector('.mlr-filemenu');
  if(menu&&!menu.querySelector('[data-act="about"]')){
    const hr=document.createElement('hr');const about=document.createElement('button');about.type='button';about.dataset.act='about';about.className='mlr-about-row';about.textContent='About';menu.append(hr,about);
    about.onclick=()=>{
      const candidates=[...document.querySelectorAll('button,a')].filter(el=>el!==about&&/^about$/i.test((el.textContent||'').trim())&&!filePop.contains(el));
      const native=candidates[0]||document.querySelector('#aboutBtn,.about-button,[data-action="about"]');
      if(native){filePop.hidden=true;native.click();return;}
      const body=document.querySelector('.about-body');if(body){alert(body.innerText);return;}
      alert(`Mesh Line Replacer\nVersion ${VERSION}`);
    };
  }
}

// Remove duplicate top/right export and About actions; FILE is authoritative.
const topbar=document.querySelector('.mlr-topbar');
const duplicateButtons=[...document.querySelectorAll('button,a')];
for(const el of duplicateButtons){
  if(filePop?.contains(el))continue;
  const txt=(el.textContent||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  const id=(el.id||'').toLowerCase();
  if(el.id==='mlrExport'||txt==='export'||txt==='export glb'||txt==='export glb…'||id==='exportbtn')el.classList.add('mlr-consolidated-hidden');
  if(txt==='about'&&!el.closest('.about-dialog,.modal,.mlr-filepop'))el.classList.add('mlr-consolidated-hidden');
}
if(topbar){const exp=topbar.querySelector('#mlrExport');if(exp)exp.remove();}

// VIEW is authoritative: move any remaining display/view controls out of the scrolling panel.
const viewPop=document.querySelector('.mlr-viewpop2');const viewList=viewPop?.querySelector('#mlrViewList');
if(viewList){
  const knownIds=['viewMode','viewTarget','viewWireOverlay','viewFaceNormals','viewVertexNormals','viewNormalLength'];
  for(const id of knownIds){const el=document.getElementById(id);if(!el)continue;const c=el.closest('.control');if(c&&!viewPop.contains(c))c.classList.add('mlr-consolidated-hidden');}
  // Hide right-panel sections that are purely Display/View after their controls moved to VIEW.
  for(const section of document.querySelectorAll('.section,section,.panel-section')){
    if(viewPop.contains(section))continue;
    const head=section.querySelector('h1,h2,h3,h4,summary');const ht=(head?.textContent||'').trim().toLowerCase();
    if(ht==='display'||ht==='view'||ht==='view modes')section.classList.add('mlr-consolidated-hidden');
  }
}

// Imported Instance mode: the actual import action lives in FILE; keep only a compact filename/status in the panel.
const customSection=document.getElementById('customInstanceSection');
if(customSection){
  const importLabel=customSection.querySelector('label[for="customInstanceFile"]');
  const importRow=importLabel?.parentElement;if(importRow)importRow.classList.add('mlr-consolidated-hidden');
  const status=document.getElementById('customMasterStatus');if(status){status.classList.add('mlr-instance-name');status.textContent=status.textContent.replace('master object','instance object');}
  const exportControl=document.getElementById('customExportMode')?.closest('.control');
  // Keep export-mode selection because it changes export structure, but tuck it into Advanced rather than duplicating an Export action.
  const advanced=[...customSection.querySelectorAll('details')].find(d=>/advanced/i.test(d.querySelector('summary')?.textContent||''));if(exportControl&&advanced)advanced.appendChild(exportControl);
}

// Hide duplicate source/import/export/about rows in scrolling panels by semantic text.
for(const c of document.querySelectorAll('.control,.section>div,.toolbar-row,.actions')){
  if(filePop?.contains(c)||viewPop?.contains(c)||topbar?.contains(c))continue;
  const t=(c.textContent||'').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim().toLowerCase();
  if(/^open source mesh/.test(t)||/^import master object/.test(t)||/^import instance object/.test(t)||(/^export glb/.test(t)&&!c.querySelector('select')))c.classList.add('mlr-consolidated-hidden');
}

// Prune empty wrappers/sections left behind by moved controls.
function pruneEmpty(){for(const section of document.querySelectorAll('.section,section,.panel-section')){if(viewPop?.contains(section)||filePop?.contains(section))continue;const visible=[...section.children].filter(ch=>!ch.classList.contains('mlr-consolidated-hidden')&&getComputedStyle(ch).display!=='none'&&!/^h[1-6]$/i.test(ch.tagName));if(!visible.length)section.classList.add('mlr-empty-section');}}
setTimeout(pruneEmpty,0);

// More aggressive context sensitivity: hide inactive size branch rather than leaving it in the scroll.
function contextRefresh(){
  const pairs=[['torusSizeMode','torusRelativeControls','torusConstantControls'],['customSizeMode','customRelativeControls','customConstantControls']];
  for(const [modeId,relId,constId] of pairs){const mode=document.getElementById(modeId),rel=document.getElementById(relId),con=document.getElementById(constId);if(!mode)continue;const isConst=mode.value==='constant';if(rel)rel.style.display=isConst?'none':'';if(con)con.style.display=isConst?'':'none';}
  pruneEmpty();
}
for(const id of ['torusSizeMode','customSizeMode','torusPlacementMode','customPlacementMode'])document.getElementById(id)?.addEventListener('change',contextRefresh);contextRefresh();
})();
