// v0.9.0-wip.7 — authoritative VIEW menu handler
(function(){
const VERSION='0.9.0-wip.7';
document.title=`Mesh Line Replacer v${VERSION}`;const vv=document.querySelector('.version');if(vv)vv.textContent=`v${VERSION}`;const av=document.querySelector('.about-version');if(av)av.textContent=`Version ${VERSION}`;
const viewBtn=document.getElementById('mlrView');const viewPop=document.querySelector('.mlr-viewpop2');const viewList=viewPop?.querySelector('#mlrViewList');const base=document.getElementById('baseViewMode')||document.getElementById('viewMode');if(!viewBtn||!viewPop||!viewList||!base)return;
function anchor(pop,btn){const r=btn.getBoundingClientRect();pop.style.left=Math.max(10,Math.min(r.left,window.innerWidth-pop.offsetWidth-10))+'px';pop.style.top=Math.max(r.bottom+6,54)+'px'}
function closeOthers(){document.querySelectorAll('.mlr-filepop,.mlr-modepop').forEach(p=>p.hidden=true)}
function refreshActive(){viewList.querySelectorAll('button[data-view-value]').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('is-active',b.dataset.viewValue===base.value))}
function rebuild(){viewList.innerHTML='';for(const opt of [...base.options]){const b=document.createElement('button');b.type='button';b.dataset.viewValue=opt.value;b.textContent=opt.textContent.trim();b.classList.toggle('is-active',opt.value===base.value);b.addEventListener('click',()=>{base.value=opt.value;base.dispatchEvent(new Event('change',{bubbles:true}));refreshActive();viewPop.hidden=true});viewList.appendChild(b)}const target=document.getElementById('viewTarget');if(target){viewList.appendChild(document.createElement('hr'));const row=document.createElement('label');row.className='mlr-menurow';const span=document.createElement('span');span.textContent='View target';row.append(span,target);viewList.appendChild(row)}const overlays=[['overlayWire','Wireframe overlay'],['overlayFaceNormals','Face normals'],['overlayVertexNormals','Vertex normals']];if(overlays.some(([id])=>document.getElementById(id))){viewList.appendChild(document.createElement('hr'));for(const [id,label] of overlays){const input=document.getElementById(id);if(!input)continue;const row=document.createElement('label');row.className='mlr-menurow mlr-check';const span=document.createElement('span');span.textContent=label;row.append(span,input);viewList.appendChild(row)}}const length=document.getElementById('normalLength');if(length){const row=document.createElement('label');row.className='mlr-menurow';const span=document.createElement('span');span.textContent='Normal length';row.append(span,length);viewList.appendChild(row)}}
const replacement=viewBtn.cloneNode(true);viewBtn.replaceWith(replacement);replacement.addEventListener('click',(e)=>{e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();const opening=viewPop.hidden;closeOthers();if(opening){rebuild();viewPop.hidden=false;requestAnimationFrame(()=>anchor(viewPop,replacement))}else viewPop.hidden=true});base.addEventListener('change',refreshActive);document.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{if(!viewPop.hidden&&!viewPop.contains(e.target)&&e.target!==replacement)viewPop.hidden=true},{capture:true});window.addEventListener('resize',()=>{if(!viewPop.hidden)anchor(viewPop,replacement)});rebuild();
})();



// v0.9.0-wip.8 — force single footer/status line and current WIP labelling
(function(){
const VERSION='0.9.0-wip.8';
document.title=`Mesh Line Replacer v${VERSION}`;
const vv=document.querySelector('.version');if(vv)vv.textContent=`v${VERSION}`;
const av=document.querySelector('.about-version');if(av)av.textContent=`Version ${VERSION}`;
// Keep only one generated status bar and make it current.
const bars=[...document.querySelectorAll('.mlr-statusbar')];
if(bars.length){const keep=bars[bars.length-1];bars.slice(0,-1).forEach(b=>b.remove());const spans=keep.querySelectorAll('span');if(spans.length)spans[spans.length-1].textContent=`v${VERSION}`;}
// Hide any legacy fixed/footer rows carrying stale WIP/version text, but preserve the current status bar.
for(const el of [...document.querySelectorAll('footer,.footer,.status-footer,.bottom-bar,.version-row')]){if(el.closest('.mlr-statusbar'))continue;const t=(el.textContent||'').toLowerCase();if(/wip|version|faces|holes|exported/.test(t))el.style.display='none';}
// Also collapse duplicate direct children fixed to bottom if they show stale WIP information.
for(const el of [...document.body.children]){if(el.classList?.contains('mlr-statusbar'))continue;const cs=getComputedStyle(el);if(cs.position==='fixed'&&/bottom/.test(el.getAttribute('style')||'')||cs.bottom==='0px'){const t=(el.textContent||'').toLowerCase();if(/wip|v0\.|faces|holes|exported/.test(t)&&!el.classList?.contains('mlr-topbar'))el.style.display='none';}}
})();



// v0.9.0-wip.9 — independent faceted shading VIEW option
(function(){
const VERSION='0.9.0-wip.9';
document.title=`Mesh Line Replacer v${VERSION}`;
const vv=document.querySelector('.version');if(vv)vv.textContent=`v${VERSION}`;
const av=document.querySelector('.about-version');if(av)av.textContent=`Version ${VERSION}`;

const viewBtn=document.getElementById('mlrView');
const viewPop=document.querySelector('.mlr-viewpop2');
const viewList=viewPop?.querySelector('#mlrViewList');
const base=document.getElementById('baseViewMode')||document.getElementById('viewMode');
if(!viewBtn||!viewPop||!viewList||!base)return;

let faceted=document.getElementById('viewFacetedShading');
if(!faceted){faceted=document.createElement('input');faceted.type='checkbox';faceted.id='viewFacetedShading';faceted.checked=false;}
const originalFlat=new WeakMap();
function materialsOf(mat){return Array.isArray(mat)?mat:[mat]}
function roots(){const arr=[];try{if(typeof sourceRoot!=='undefined'&&sourceRoot)arr.push(sourceRoot)}catch{}try{if(typeof resultRoot!=='undefined'&&resultRoot)arr.push(resultRoot)}catch{}return arr}
function applyFaceted(){
 const on=faceted.checked;
 for(const root of roots())root.traverse?.(obj=>{
   if(!obj.isMesh&&!obj.isInstancedMesh)return;
   for(const mat of materialsOf(obj.material)){
     if(!mat||!('flatShading' in mat))continue;
     if(!originalFlat.has(mat))originalFlat.set(mat,!!mat.flatShading);
     mat.flatShading=on?true:originalFlat.get(mat);
     mat.needsUpdate=true;
   }
 });
}
faceted.addEventListener('change',applyFaceted);

function anchor(pop,btn){const r=btn.getBoundingClientRect();pop.style.left=Math.max(10,Math.min(r.left,window.innerWidth-pop.offsetWidth-10))+'px';pop.style.top=Math.max(r.bottom+6,54)+'px'}
function closeOthers(){document.querySelectorAll('.mlr-filepop,.mlr-modepop').forEach(p=>p.hidden=true)}
function refreshActive(){viewList.querySelectorAll('button[data-view-value]').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('is-active',b.dataset.viewValue===base.value))}
function menuCheck(input,label){const row=document.createElement('label');row.className='mlr-menurow mlr-check';const span=document.createElement('span');span.textContent=label;row.append(span,input);return row}
function rebuild(){
 viewList.innerHTML='';
 for(const opt of [...base.options]){
   const b=document.createElement('button');b.type='button';b.dataset.viewValue=opt.value;b.textContent=opt.textContent.trim();b.classList.toggle('is-active',opt.value===base.value);
   b.onclick=()=>{base.value=opt.value;base.dispatchEvent(new Event('change',{bubbles:true}));refreshActive();setTimeout(applyFaceted,0);viewPop.hidden=true};viewList.appendChild(b);
 }
 const target=document.getElementById('viewTarget');
 if(target){viewList.appendChild(document.createElement('hr'));const row=document.createElement('label');row.className='mlr-menurow';const span=document.createElement('span');span.textContent='View target';row.append(span,target);viewList.appendChild(row)}
 viewList.appendChild(document.createElement('hr'));
 viewList.appendChild(menuCheck(faceted,'Faceted shading'));
 const overlays=[['overlayWire','Wireframe overlay'],['overlayFaceNormals','Face normals'],['overlayVertexNormals','Vertex normals']];
 for(const [id,label] of overlays){const input=document.getElementById(id);if(input)viewList.appendChild(menuCheck(input,label))}
 const length=document.getElementById('normalLength');if(length){const row=document.createElement('label');row.className='mlr-menurow';const span=document.createElement('span');span.textContent='Normal length';row.append(span,length);viewList.appendChild(row)}
}

const replacement=viewBtn.cloneNode(true);viewBtn.replaceWith(replacement);
replacement.addEventListener('click',e=>{e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();const opening=viewPop.hidden;closeOthers();if(opening){rebuild();viewPop.hidden=false;requestAnimationFrame(()=>anchor(viewPop,replacement))}else viewPop.hidden=true});
base.addEventListener('change',()=>{refreshActive();setTimeout(applyFaceted,0)});
document.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{if(!viewPop.hidden&&!viewPop.contains(e.target)&&e.target!==replacement)viewPop.hidden=true},{capture:true});
window.addEventListener('resize',()=>{if(!viewPop.hidden)anchor(viewPop,replacement)});
for(const id of ['generateBtn','generate','buildBtn'])document.getElementById(id)?.addEventListener('click',()=>setTimeout(applyFaceted,60));
const mode=document.getElementById('replaceMode')||document.getElementById('mlrMode');mode?.addEventListener('change',()=>setTimeout(applyFaceted,30));
rebuild();applyFaceted();
})();



// v0.9.0-wip.10 — safe compact mode parameter grouping
(function(){
const VERSION='0.9.0-wip.10';
try{
  document.title=`Mesh Line Replacer v${VERSION}`;
  const vv=document.querySelector('.version'); if(vv) vv.textContent=`v${VERSION}`;
  const av=document.querySelector('.about-version'); if(av) av.textContent=`Version ${VERSION}`;
  const panel=document.querySelector('.panel,.sidebar,.controls'); if(!panel) return;
  const style=document.createElement('style');
  style.textContent=`.mlr10-section{border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.09);border-radius:10px;margin:7px 0;background:rgba(255,255,255,.025);overflow:hidden}.mlr10-section>summary{list-style:none;cursor:pointer;padding:8px 10px;font-weight:650;font-size:12px;letter-spacing:.02em;text-transform:uppercase;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between}.mlr10-section>summary::-webkit-details-marker{display:none}.mlr10-section>summary:after{content:'›';opacity:.55}.mlr10-section[open]>summary:after{transform:rotate(90deg)}.mlr10-body{padding:1px 8px 8px}.mlr10-hidden{display:none!important}.mlr10-pair{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:5px 8px}.mlr10-pair>.control{min-width:0}.mlr10-section .control{margin:5px 0!important}@media(max-width:759px),(orientation:portrait){.mlr10-pair{display:block}}`;
  document.head.appendChild(style);
  const txt=c=>(c?.textContent||'').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim().toLowerCase();
  const ids=c=>[...(c?.querySelectorAll?.('[id]')||[])].map(e=>e.id).join(' ').toLowerCase();
  const oldSections=[...panel.querySelectorAll('.mlr-section')];
  const candidates=[];
  for(const sec of oldSections){
    for(const c of sec.querySelectorAll('.control,label.check')) if(!c.closest('.mlr-pop,.mlr-filepop,.mlr-viewpop2')) candidates.push(c);
  }
  // Add any direct controls not already captured.
  for(const c of panel.querySelectorAll(':scope > .control,:scope > label.check')) if(!candidates.includes(c)) candidates.push(c);
  const forbidden=/view mode|base view|wireframe overlay|face normals|vertex normals|normal length|view target|open source|import master|import instance|save project|load project|quick save|quick load|project info|about|export glb|instance export|^export$|generate/i;
  function category(c){const t=txt(c)+' '+ids(c);if(/symmetr/.test(t))return'Symmetry';if(/density|placement|face centre|face center|shared.*node|shared.*vertex|normal offset|normal direction|force outward/.test(t))return'Placement';if(/size mode|relative|constant|scale|major radius|tube radius|ring size|tube size|radius|diameter|thickness|inset width|width/.test(t))return'Size';if(/tilt|orientation|alignment|rotation|rotate|axis/.test(t))return'Orientation';if(/segment|sides|bevel|repair|weld|manifold|tolerance|watertight|flat shading|quality|junction|mitre|miter/.test(t))return'Geometry / Quality';return'General'}
  const groups=new Map();
  for(const c of candidates){if(forbidden.test(txt(c))) {c.classList.add('mlr10-hidden'); continue;} const g=category(c); if(!groups.has(g)) groups.set(g,[]); groups.get(g).push(c);}
  // Remove any prior WIP10 shell only. Never unwrap legacy sections.
  for(const d of [...panel.querySelectorAll(':scope > .mlr10-section')]) d.remove();
  const order=['General','Symmetry','Placement','Size','Orientation','Geometry / Quality'];
  for(const name of order){const items=groups.get(name);if(!items?.length) continue;const d=document.createElement('details');d.className='mlr10-section';d.dataset.mlr10=name;d.open=name!=='Geometry / Quality';const s=document.createElement('summary');s.textContent=name;const b=document.createElement('div');b.className='mlr10-body';d.append(s,b);for(const c of items)b.appendChild(c);panel.appendChild(d);}
  // Hide only the now-empty legacy section wrappers.
  for(const sec of oldSections){const remaining=[...sec.querySelectorAll('.control,label.check')].filter(c=>!c.classList.contains('mlr10-hidden'));if(!remaining.length)sec.classList.add('mlr10-hidden');}
  function pair(section,ra,rb){const body=panel.querySelector(`.mlr10-section[data-mlr10="${section}"] .mlr10-body`);if(!body)return;const cs=[...body.children].filter(x=>x.classList?.contains('control'));const a=cs.find(c=>ra.test(txt(c)+' '+ids(c))),b=cs.find(c=>rb.test(txt(c)+' '+ids(c))&&c!==a);if(a&&b&&a.parentElement===body&&b.parentElement===body){const row=document.createElement('div');row.className='mlr10-pair';body.insertBefore(row,a);row.append(a,b);}}
  pair('Orientation',/tilt x|tiltx/,/tilt y|tilty/);
  pair('Size',/major radius|major.*size|major.*scale/,/tube radius|tube.*size|tube.*scale/);
  pair('Geometry / Quality',/tolerance/,/manifold|watertight/);
  function refreshSections(){for(const d of panel.querySelectorAll(':scope > .mlr10-section')){const controls=[...d.querySelectorAll('.control,label.check')];const visible=controls.some(c=>getComputedStyle(c).display!=='none'&&!c.classList.contains('mlr10-hidden')&&!c.classList.contains('mlr-wip6-hidden')&&!c.classList.contains('mlr-consolidated-hidden')&&!c.classList.contains('mlr-mode-hidden')&&!c.classList.contains('mlr-context-hidden'));d.classList.toggle('mlr10-hidden',!visible);}}
  for(const e of panel.querySelectorAll('select,input[type=radio],input[type=checkbox]')) e.addEventListener('change',()=>setTimeout(refreshSections,0));
  setTimeout(refreshSections,0);
  for(const el of document.querySelectorAll('.mlr-statusbar,.mlr-wip8-statusbar')){const spans=el.querySelectorAll('span');if(spans.length)spans[spans.length-1].textContent=`v${VERSION}`;}
}catch(err){console.error('WIP10 grouping skipped safely:',err);}
})();



// v0.9.0-wip.11 — compact panel refinement over proven WIP10
(function(){
const VERSION='0.9.0-wip.11';
document.title=`Mesh Line Replacer v${VERSION}`;
const vv=document.querySelector('.version');if(vv)vv.textContent=`v${VERSION}`;
const av=document.querySelector('.about-version');if(av)av.textContent=`Version ${VERSION}`;
const panel=document.querySelector('.panel,.sidebar,.controls');if(!panel)return;
const style=document.createElement('style');style.textContent=`
.mlr11-inline{border:0!important;background:transparent!important;margin:3px 0!important;overflow:visible!important}.mlr11-inline>summary{display:none!important}.mlr11-inline>.mlr10-body{padding:0!important}.mlr11-hidden{display:none!important}.mlr11-section-note{font-size:11px;opacity:.62;margin:0 2px 5px}.mlr10-section>summary{padding:7px 9px!important}.mlr10-body{padding:0 7px 7px!important}.mlr10-section .control{margin:4px 0!important}.mlr11-pair{display:grid;grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1fr) minmax(0,1fr);gap:4px 7px}.mlr11-pair>.control{min-width:0}@media(max-width:759px),(orientation:portrait){.mlr11-pair{display:block}}
`;document.head.appendChild(style);
function txt(el){return(el?.textContent||'').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim().toLowerCase()}
function ids(el){return[...el.querySelectorAll?.('[id]')||[]].map(x=>x.id).join(' ').toLowerCase()}
function visibleControls(sec){return[...sec.querySelectorAll('.control,label.check')].filter(c=>{const s=getComputedStyle(c);return s.display!=='none'&&!c.classList.contains('mlr10-hidden')&&!c.classList.contains('mlr11-hidden')&&!c.classList.contains('mlr-consolidated-hidden')&&!c.classList.contains('mlr-wip6-hidden')})}
function pairWithin(sec,tests){const body=sec.querySelector('.mlr10-body');if(!body)return;for(const [aRx,bRx] of tests){const cs=[...body.children].filter(x=>x.classList?.contains('control')&&!x.closest('.mlr11-pair,.mlr10-pair'));const a=cs.find(c=>aRx.test(txt(c)+' '+ids(c)));const b=cs.find(c=>c!==a&&bRx.test(txt(c)+' '+ids(c)));if(a&&b){const row=document.createElement('div');row.className='mlr11-pair';body.insertBefore(row,a);row.append(a,b)}}}
function collapseSingleControlHeadings(){for(const sec of panel.querySelectorAll('.mlr10-section')){const cs=visibleControls(sec);if(cs.length===1&&sec.dataset.mlr10!=='Geometry / Quality'){sec.classList.add('mlr11-inline');sec.open=true}else sec.classList.remove('mlr11-inline')}}
function activeModeText(){const e=document.getElementById('replaceMode')||document.getElementById('mlrMode');return((e?.value||'')+' '+(e?.selectedOptions?.[0]?.textContent||'')).toLowerCase()}
function context(){const mode=activeModeText();
 // Hide controls from unrelated generator families.
 for(const sec of panel.querySelectorAll('.mlr10-section')){for(const c of sec.querySelectorAll('.control,label.check')){const key=ids(c)+' '+txt(c);let hide=false;if(/torus/.test(key)&&!mode.includes('torus'))hide=true;if(/custom|imported instance|master object/.test(key)&&!(mode.includes('custom')||mode.includes('import')))hide=true;c.classList.toggle('mlr11-hidden',hide)}}
 // Size mode: show only the active branch.
 for(const prefix of ['torus','custom']){const sm=document.getElementById(prefix+'SizeMode');if(!sm)continue;const constant=sm.value==='constant';for(const c of panel.querySelectorAll('.control')){const key=ids(c)+' '+txt(c);if(!key.includes(prefix))continue;const isConst=/constant/.test(key);const isRel=/relative|scale/.test(key)&&!isConst;if(isConst)c.classList.toggle('mlr11-hidden',!constant);if(isRel)c.classList.toggle('mlr11-hidden',constant)}}
 // Placement mode: hide node-only helpers unless node placement is active.
 for(const prefix of ['torus','custom']){const pm=document.getElementById(prefix+'PlacementMode');if(!pm)continue;const node=pm.value==='node';for(const c of panel.querySelectorAll('.control')){const key=ids(c)+' '+txt(c);if(!key.includes(prefix))continue;if(/node normal|averaged normal|shared.*node|shared.*vertex/.test(key)&&!key.includes('placementmode'))c.classList.toggle('mlr11-hidden',!node)}}
 for(const sec of panel.querySelectorAll('.mlr10-section')){const cs=visibleControls(sec);sec.classList.toggle('mlr11-hidden',cs.length===0);if(sec.dataset.mlr10==='Geometry / Quality')sec.open=false}
 collapseSingleControlHeadings();
}
for(const sec of panel.querySelectorAll('.mlr10-section')){if(sec.dataset.mlr10==='Orientation')pairWithin(sec,[[/tilt x|tiltx/,/tilt y|tilty/],[/rotation x|rotate x/,/rotation y|rotate y/]]);if(sec.dataset.mlr10==='Size')pairWithin(sec,[[/major radius|major.*size|major.*scale/,/tube radius|tube.*size|tube.*scale/],[/ring segments?/,/tube segments?/]]);if(sec.dataset.mlr10==='Geometry / Quality')pairWithin(sec,[[/weld tolerance|tolerance/,/manifold|watertight/],[/bevel/,/thickness|width/]])}
for(const id of ['replaceMode','mlrMode','torusSizeMode','customSizeMode','torusPlacementMode','customPlacementMode'])document.getElementById(id)?.addEventListener('change',()=>requestAnimationFrame(context));
context();
// Keep one footer only and label it with the current WIP.
const bars=[...document.querySelectorAll('.mlr-statusbar,.mlr-wip8-statusbar')];bars.slice(1).forEach(x=>x.remove());if(bars[0]){const sp=bars[0].querySelectorAll('span');if(sp.length)sp[sp.length-1].textContent=`v${VERSION}`}
})();



// v0.9.0-wip.12 — compact inspector navigation / accordion
(function(){
const VERSION='0.9.0-wip.12';
document.title=`Mesh Line Replacer v${VERSION}`;
const vv=document.querySelector('.version');if(vv)vv.textContent=`v${VERSION}`;
const av=document.querySelector('.about-version');if(av)av.textContent=`Version ${VERSION}`;
const panel=document.querySelector('.panel,.sidebar,.controls');if(!panel)return;
const sections=[...panel.querySelectorAll('.mlr10-section')];if(!sections.length)return;
const style=document.createElement('style');style.textContent=`
.mlr12-head{position:sticky;top:0;z-index:30;margin:0 0 7px;padding:7px;background:rgba(25,28,34,.97);backdrop-filter:blur(12px);border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.09);border-radius:10px}.mlr12-title{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:8px;margin:0 1px 6px;font-size:11px;color:#aeb7c5}.mlr12-title strong{color:#f3f5f8;font-size:12px}.mlr12-nav{display:flex;gap:4px;overflow-x:auto;scrollbar-width:none}.mlr12-nav::-webkit-scrollbar{display:none}.mlr12-nav button{flex:0 0 auto;min-height:30px;padding:0 8px;border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.1);border-radius:7px;background:#292e38;color:#cdd3dd;font:600 10px -apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,"Segoe UI",sans-serif}.mlr12-nav button.is-active{background:#496da8;color:#fff;border-color:#6485bc}.mlr12-section-hidden{display:none!important}.mlr12-accordion .mlr10-section:not([open])>.mlr10-body{display:none!important}.mlr12-accordion .mlr10-section>summary{position:relative}.mlr12-mode{white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;max-width:180px}.mlr12-compact-toggle{border:0;background:transparent;color:#aeb7c5;padding:0;font:600 10px -apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,"Segoe UI",sans-serif}
@media(max-width:759px),(orientation:portrait){.mlr12-head{position:relative}.mlr12-nav button{min-height:34px}}
`;document.head.appendChild(style);panel.classList.add('mlr12-accordion');
const head=document.createElement('div');head.className='mlr12-head';head.innerHTML=`<div class="mlr12-title"><strong>INSPECTOR</strong><span class="mlr12-mode"></span><button type="button" class="mlr12-compact-toggle">EXPAND ALL</button></div><div class="mlr12-nav"></div>`;
const firstSection=sections[0];firstSection.parentNode.insertBefore(head,firstSection);const nav=head.querySelector('.mlr12-nav'),modeLabel=head.querySelector('.mlr12-mode'),toggle=head.querySelector('.mlr12-compact-toggle');
const names={'General':'General','Symmetry':'Symmetry','Placement':'Placement','Size':'Size','Orientation':'Orientation','Geometry / Quality':'Quality'};let accordion=true;
function visibleSection(sec){const st=getComputedStyle(sec);return st.display!=='none'&&!sec.classList.contains('mlr10-hidden')&&!sec.classList.contains('mlr11-hidden')&&!sec.classList.contains('mlr12-section-hidden')}
function sectionName(sec){return sec.dataset.mlr10||sec.querySelector('summary')?.textContent?.trim()||'Section'}
function modeText(){const e=document.getElementById('replaceMode')||document.getElementById('mlrMode');return(e?.selectedOptions?.[0]?.textContent||e?.value||'').trim()}
function openSection(sec,scroll=true){if(!sec||!visibleSection(sec))return;if(accordion)for(const s of sections)if(s!==sec&&visibleSection(s))s.open=false;sec.open=true;syncNav();if(scroll){const y=sec.getBoundingClientRect().top-panel.getBoundingClientRect().top+panel.scrollTop-head.offsetHeight-6;try{panel.scrollTo({top:Math.max(0,y),behavior:'smooth'})}catch{panel.scrollTop=Math.max(0,y)}}}
function syncNav(){modeLabel.textContent=modeText();const visible=sections.filter(visibleSection);nav.innerHTML='';for(const sec of visible){const name=sectionName(sec);const b=document.createElement('button');b.type='button';b.textContent=names[name]||name;b.classList.toggle('is-active',sec.open);b.onclick=()=>openSection(sec,true);nav.appendChild(b)}toggle.textContent=accordion?'EXPAND ALL':'COMPACT';}
function enforce(){const visible=sections.filter(visibleSection);if(!visible.length){syncNav();return}if(accordion){let opened=visible.filter(s=>s.open);if(opened.length>1){const keep=opened[0];opened.slice(1).forEach(s=>s.open=false)}else if(!opened.length){const preferred=visible.find(s=>sectionName(s)==='Placement')||visible.find(s=>sectionName(s)==='Size')||visible[0];preferred.open=true}}syncNav()}
for(const sec of sections){sec.addEventListener('toggle',()=>{if(!sec.open||!accordion)return;for(const other of sections)if(other!==sec&&visibleSection(other))other.open=false;syncNav()})}
toggle.onclick=()=>{accordion=!accordion;if(!accordion){sections.filter(visibleSection).forEach(s=>s.open=true)}else enforce();syncNav()};

// v0.9.0-wip.13 — keep faceted shading exclusively in VIEW
(function(){
const VERSION='0.9.0-wip.13';

function rowOf(el){return el.closest('.mlr-menurow')||el.closest('.control')||el.closest('label.check')||el.parentElement}
function ensureViewHome(){
  const row=rowOf(faceted);
  if(viewList&&row&&!viewList.contains(row)){
    const label=document.createElement('label');label.className='mlr-menurow mlr-check';
    const span=document.createElement('span');span.textContent='Faceted shading';
    label.append(span,faceted);viewList.appendChild(label);
  }
  if(panel){
    for(const c of panel.querySelectorAll('.control,label.check,.mlr10-section,.mlr-section')){
      if(c.contains(faceted))continue;
      const t=(c.textContent||'').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim().toLowerCase();
      if(t==='faceted shading'||(/^faceted shading\b/.test(t)&&!viewPop?.contains(c)))c.style.setProperty('display','none','important');
    }
    for(const sec of panel.querySelectorAll('.mlr10-section,.mlr-section')){
      const visible=[...sec.querySelectorAll('.control,label.check')].some(c=>getComputedStyle(c).display!=='none');
      if(!visible)sec.style.setProperty('display','none','important');
    }
  }
}
ensureViewHome();

// v0.9.0-wip.14 — explicit mode ownership sweep for inspector controls
(function(){

function txt(el){return(el?.textContent||'').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim().toLowerCase()}function ids(el){return[...el.querySelectorAll?.('[id]')||[]].map(x=>x.id).join(' ').toLowerCase()}function key(el){return ids(el)+' '+txt(el)}
function mode(){const e=document.getElementById('replaceMode')||document.getElementById('mlrMode');const s=((e?.value||'')+' '+(e?.selectedOptions?.[0]?.textContent||'')).toLowerCase();if(/torus/.test(s))return'torus';if(/import|custom|instance object/.test(s))return'custom';if(/solid/.test(s))return'solid';if(/inset|surface web/.test(s))return'inset';if(/tube|cylinder|faceted/.test(s))return'tubes';return'other'}
const globalRx=/symmetr|placement|density|normal offset|force outward|size mode|relative|constant|scale|tilt|orientation|alignment|rotation/;
const ownership=[
 {rx:/hub detail|hubdetail|hub radius|hub size|junction hub|node hub/,m:['tubes']},
 {rx:/junction|mitre|miter|tube sides|cylinder sides|tube radius|edge tube|faceted tube/,m:['tubes']},
 {rx:/inset|surface web|web width|inset width/,m:['inset','solid']},
 {rx:/solid web|solid surface|solidify|wall thickness|bevel|inside corner|outside corner/,m:['solid']},
 {rx:/torus|major radius|ring radius|ring segments|tube segments/,m:['torus']},
 {rx:/master object|instance object|custom instance|imported instance|repair|manifold|watertight|weld tolerance|master mesh|alignment axis/,m:['custom']},
 {rx:/export mode|grouped instance|instance export/,m:['custom','torus']},
 {rx:/faceted shading|base view|view mode|view target|wireframe|face normals|vertex normals|normal length|mesh analysis/,m:[]}
];
function allowed(c,m){const k=key(c);for(const rule of ownership)if(rule.rx.test(k))return rule.m.includes(m);if(globalRx.test(k))return true;return true}

 for(const c of panel.querySelectorAll('.control,label.check')){const hide=!allowed(c,m);c.classList.toggle('mlr14-hidden',hide)}

 // keep inspector navigator honest: hide buttons for empty sections.
 document.querySelectorAll('.mlr12-nav button,[data-mlr12-target]').forEach(b=>{const n=b.dataset.mlr12Target||b.dataset.target||'';const sec=n?panel.querySelector(`[data-mlr10="${CSS.escape(n)}"]`):null;if(sec)b.classList.toggle('mlr14-hidden',sec.classList.contains('mlr14-hidden'))});
}

refresh();
const bars=[...document.querySelectorAll('.mlr-statusbar,.mlr-wip8-statusbar')];bars.slice(1).forEach(x=>x.remove());if(bars[0]){const sp=bars[0].querySelectorAll('span');if(sp.length)sp[sp.length-1].textContent=`v${VERSION}`}

function isTorus(){const e=document.getElementById('replaceMode')||document.getElementById('mlrMode');const s=((e?.value||'')+' '+(e?.selectedOptions?.[0]?.textContent||'')).toLowerCase();return /torus/.test(s)}
function control(id){const e=document.getElementById(id);return e?.closest('.control')||e?.closest('label.check')||null}
function reveal(el){if(!el)return;HIDE_CLASSES.forEach(c=>el.classList.remove(c));el.classList.remove('mlr20-hidden');el.style.removeProperty('display')}
function conceal(el){if(!el)return;el.classList.add('mlr20-hidden');el.style.setProperty('display','none','important')}

function applyTorusPlacementOrientation(){if(!isTorus())return;['torusPlacementMode','torusDensity','torusNormalMode','torusNormalOffset'].forEach(id=>move(id,'Placement'));['torusAxis','torusTiltX','torusTiltY'].forEach(id=>move(id,'Orientation'))}

function updateNavigator(){for(const name of ['Placement','Size','Orientation']){const s=section(name);if(!s)continue;document.querySelectorAll('.mlr12-nav button,[data-mlr12-target]').forEach(b=>{const t=b.dataset.mlr12Target||b.dataset.target||'';if(t===name)reveal(b)})}}
function stamp(){document.title=`Mesh Line Replacer v${VERSION}`;document.querySelectorAll('.version').forEach(x=>x.textContent=`v${VERSION}`);const av=document.querySelector('.about-version');if(av)av.textContent=`Version ${VERSION}`;const bars=[...document.querySelectorAll('.mlr-statusbar,.mlr-wip8-statusbar')];bars.slice(1).forEach(x=>x.remove());if(bars[0]){const sp=bars[0].querySelectorAll('span');if(sp.length)sp[sp.length-1].textContent=`v${VERSION}`}}

document.getElementById('torusSizeMode')?.addEventListener('change',()=>setTimeout(refresh,0));
for(const id of ['replaceMode','mlrMode'])document.getElementById(id)?.addEventListener('change',()=>setTimeout(refresh,0));
for(const id of ['generateBtn','generate','buildBtn'])document.getElementById(id)?.addEventListener('click',()=>setTimeout(refresh,100));
requestAnimationFrame(refresh);
})();

// v0.9.0-wip.22 — preserve imported master object's authored origin/pivot
(function(){
const VERSION='0.9.0-wip.22';

async function sourceMasterBoundsCentre(file){
  const ext=file.name.split('.').pop()?.toLowerCase();
  let root=null;
  let url=null;
  try{
    if(ext==='obj'){
      root=new OBJLoader().parse(await file.text());
    }else{
      url=URL.createObjectURL(file);
      if(ext==='glb'||ext==='gltf') root=(await new GLTFLoader().loadAsync(url)).scene;
      else if(ext==='stl') root=new THREE.Mesh(await new STLLoader().loadAsync(url),new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial());
      else return null;
    }
    root.updateMatrixWorld(true);
    const bounds=new THREE.Box3();
    let found=false;
    root.traverse(obj=>{
      if(!obj.isMesh||!obj.geometry?.attributes?.position)return;
      const pos=obj.geometry.getAttribute('position');
      for(let i=0;i<pos.count;i++){
        bounds.expandByPoint(new THREE.Vector3(pos.getX(i),pos.getY(i),pos.getZ(i)).applyMatrix4(obj.matrixWorld));
        found=true;
      }
    });
    return found&&!bounds.isEmpty()?bounds.getCenter(new THREE.Vector3()):null;
  }finally{
    if(url)URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    if(root){
      root.traverse(obj=>{
        obj.geometry?.dispose?.();
        const m=obj.material;
        if(Array.isArray(m))m.forEach(x=>x?.dispose?.()); else m?.dispose?.();
      });
    }
  }
}

// The legacy importer recentres repaired geometry around its bounding-box centre.
// Wrap it and restore that exact centre afterwards so local (0,0,0) remains the authored pivot.
const legacyLoadCustomMasterFile=loadCustomMasterFile;
loadCustomMasterFile=async function(file){
  let centre=null;
  try{ centre=await sourceMasterBoundsCentre(file); }catch(err){ console.warn('Could not inspect imported master origin; using legacy centring.',err); }
  await legacyLoadCustomMasterFile(file);
  if(centre&&customMasterRecords?.length){
    for(const rec of customMasterRecords){
      rec.geometry.translate(centre.x,centre.y,centre.z);
      rec.geometry.computeBoundingBox();
      rec.geometry.computeBoundingSphere();
    }
    if(customMasterStatus) customMasterStatus.textContent += ' • source origin preserved';
  }
};

function stamp(){
  document.title=`Mesh Line Replacer v${VERSION}`;
  document.querySelectorAll('.version').forEach(x=>x.textContent=`v${VERSION}`);
  const av=document.querySelector('.about-version');if(av)av.textContent=`Version ${VERSION}`;
  const bars=[...document.querySelectorAll('.mlr-statusbar,.mlr-wip8-statusbar')];
  bars.slice(1).forEach(x=>x.remove());
  if(bars[0]){const sp=bars[0].querySelectorAll('span');if(sp.length)sp[sp.length-1].textContent=`v${VERSION}`;}
}
requestAnimationFrame(stamp);
})();

document.querySelectorAll('.version').forEach(x=>x.textContent=`v${VERSION}`);

addControls('torus');addControls('custom');

let cachedFaces=null,cachedSignature='';
function faceCentre(face){return face.reduce((a,p)=>a.add(p),new THREE.Vector3()).multiplyScalar(1/Math.max(face.length,1))}
function collectContext(){
 try{
   const result=typeof collectFaces==='function'?collectFaces():null;let faces=result?.faces||[];
   if(!faces.length)return[];
   if(typeof orientTorusFaces==='function')faces=orientTorusFaces(faces);
   const sig=`${faces.length}:${faces[0]?.length||0}:${faces[0]?.[0]?.x||0}`;
   if(sig===cachedSignature&&cachedFaces)return cachedFaces;
   cachedSignature=sig;
   cachedFaces=faces.map(face=>{const centre=faceCentre(face),normal=typeof polygonNormal==='function'?polygonNormal(face):null;if(!normal)return null;const frame=nativeFrame(face,normal.clone(),centre.clone());return frame?{face,centre,normal:frame.normal.clone().normalize(),xAxis:frame.xAxis.clone().normalize()}:null}).filter(Boolean);
   return cachedFaces;
 }catch(e){console.warn('MeshUtilz smoothing context failed',e);return[]}
}
function strength(){const m=currentMode(),p=m==='custom'?'custom':'torus';const field=document.getElementById(p+'OrientationField');if(field?.value!=='smooth')return 0;return Math.max(0,Math.min(1,Number(document.getElementById(p+'OrientationSmooth')?.value||0)/100))}
function smoothFrame(base,point){
 const amount=strength();if(amount<=0)return base;
 const context=collectContext();if(context.length<2)return base;
 // Find a small spatial neighbourhood. Limiting to 12 keeps this prototype responsive on denser meshes.
 const near=context.map(r=>({r,d:r.centre.distanceToSquared(point)})).sort((a,b)=>a.d-b.d).slice(0,12);
 const baseN=base.normal.clone().normalize(),baseX=base.xAxis.clone().normalize();
 const avgN=new THREE.Vector3(),avgX=new THREE.Vector3();let wsum=0;
 for(const {r,d} of near){
   const w=1/(Math.sqrt(d)+1e-6);const n=r.normal.clone();if(n.dot(baseN)<0)n.negate();let x=r.xAxis.clone();if(x.dot(baseX)<0)x.negate();avgN.addScaledVector(n,w);avgX.addScaledVector(x,w);wsum+=w;
 }
 if(wsum<=0||avgN.lengthSq()<1e-12)return base;
 avgN.normalize();
 const n=baseN.clone().lerp(avgN,amount).normalize();
 // Tangent averaging removes rotational chatter. Project back into the smoothed normal plane.
 avgX.multiplyScalar(1/wsum);avgX.addScaledVector(n,-avgX.dot(n));if(avgX.lengthSq()<1e-12){avgX.copy(baseX).addScaledVector(n,-baseX.dot(n))}avgX.normalize();
 let x=baseX.clone();x.addScaledVector(n,-x.dot(n));if(x.lengthSq()<1e-12)x.copy(avgX);else x.normalize();x.lerp(avgX,amount);x.addScaledVector(n,-x.dot(n));if(x.lengthSq()<1e-12)x.copy(avgX);x.normalize();
 const y=n.clone().cross(x).normalize();
 return{xAxis:x,yAxis:y,normal:n};
}
// Wrap the existing frame builders, leaving placement positions, scaling, tilt and axis correction untouched.
torusLocalFrame=function(face,normal,centre){const base=nativeFrame(face,normal,centre);if(!base||strength()<=0)return base;return smoothFrame(base,centre||faceCentre(face))};
torusNodeFrame=function(node){const base=nativeNodeFrame(node);if(!base||strength()<=0||!node?.point)return base;return smoothFrame(base,node.point)};

function applyUI(){const m=currentMode();for(const c of document.querySelectorAll('.muz-orient-control')){const show=c.dataset.muzMode===m;c.classList.toggle('mlr24-hidden',!show);c.style.display=show?'':'none'}if(m==='torus'||m==='custom'){const body=orientationBody();for(const c of document.querySelectorAll(`.muz-orient-control[data-muz-mode="${m}"]`))body.prepend(c)}stamp()}
function stamp(){document.title=`${PRODUCT} v${VERSION}`;document.querySelectorAll('.version').forEach(x=>x.textContent=`v${VERSION}`);const av=document.querySelector('.about-version');if(av)av.textContent=`${PRODUCT} · Version ${VERSION}`;const title=document.querySelector('.title');if(title)title.textContent=PRODUCT;const bars=[...document.querySelectorAll('.mlr-statusbar,.mlr-wip8-statusbar')];bars.slice(1).forEach(x=>x.remove());if(bars[0]){const sp=bars[0].querySelectorAll('span');if(sp.length)sp[sp.length-1].textContent=`v${VERSION}`}}
function settle(){cachedSignature='';cachedFaces=null;applyUI();requestAnimationFrame(applyUI);setTimeout(applyUI,160)}
for(const id of ['replaceMode','mlrMode'])document.getElementById(id)?.addEventListener('change',settle);
for(const id of ['generateBtn','generate','buildBtn','mlrGenerate'])document.getElementById(id)?.addEventListener('click',()=>{cachedSignature='';cachedFaces=null;setTimeout(stamp,150)});
requestAnimationFrame(settle);
})();

function apply(){
 const m=activeMode();
 const torus=['torusOrientationField','torusOrientationSmooth','torusAxis','torusTiltX','torusTiltY'];
 const custom=['customOrientationField','customOrientationSmooth','customAxis','customTiltX','customTiltY'];

 document.querySelectorAll('.mlr12-nav button,[data-mlr12-target]').forEach(b=>{const t=b.dataset.mlr12Target||b.dataset.target||'';if(t==='Orientation')show(b)});

}

// MeshUtilz v0.9.1-wip.4 — independent source-mesh wireframe overlay
(function(){

const helperRoot=new THREE.Group();helperRoot.name='MeshUtilzSourceWireframe';scene.add(helperRoot);
function disposeHelper(obj){obj.traverse?.(c=>{c.geometry?.dispose?.();if(Array.isArray(c.material))c.material.forEach(m=>m?.dispose?.());else c.material?.dispose?.()});obj.removeFromParent?.()}

function addWireForMesh(mesh,matrixOverride=null){if(!mesh?.geometry)return;const geo=new THREE.WireframeGeometry(mesh.geometry);const mat=new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({color:0x101318,transparent:true,opacity:0.88,depthTest:false,depthWrite:false});const line=new THREE.LineSegments(geo,mat);line.renderOrder=999;line.matrixAutoUpdate=false;if(matrixOverride)line.matrix.multiplyMatrices(mesh.matrixWorld,matrixOverride);else line.matrix.copy(mesh.matrixWorld);helperRoot.add(line)}

toggle.addEventListener('change',rebuild);

// MeshUtilz v0.9.1-wip.5 — Nomad facegroup-boundary placement prototype
(function(){
const PRODUCT='MeshUtilz', VERSION='0.9.1-wip.5';
if(typeof THREE==='undefined'||typeof fileInput==='undefined')return;
let glbPrimitiveGroups=[];

function parseGlbPrimitiveGroups(file){
  if(!file||!file.name?.toLowerCase().endsWith('.glb')){glbPrimitiveGroups=[];return;}
  file.arrayBuffer().then(buf=>{
    try{
      const dv=new DataView(buf); if(dv.getUint32(0,true)!==0x46546c67)return;
      let off=12,json=null;
      while(off+8<=buf.byteLength){const len=dv.getUint32(off,true),type=dv.getUint32(off+4,true);off+=8;if(type===0x4e4f534a){json=JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(new Uint8Array(buf,off,len)).replace(/\0+$/,''));break}off+=len;}
      const groups=[];for(const mesh of json?.meshes||[])for(const prim of mesh.primitives||[])groups.push(prim?.extras?.nomad?.group ?? null);
      glbPrimitiveGroups=groups;
    }catch(e){console.warn('MeshUtilz facegroup metadata parse failed',e);glbPrimitiveGroups=[];}
  });
}
fileInput.addEventListener('change',()=>{const f=fileInput.files?.[0];if(f)parseGlbPrimitiveGroups(f);},true);

function addPlacementOption(select){if(select&&![...select.options].some(o=>o.value==='facegroupBoundary'))select.add(new Option('Facegroup boundary','facegroupBoundary'));}
addPlacementOption(document.getElementById('torusPlacementMode'));addPlacementOption(document.getElementById('customPlacementMode'));

function makeControl(id,label,html){let e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)return e.closest('.control')||e;const c=document.createElement('div');c.className='control muz-fg-control';c.innerHTML=`<label for="${id}">${label}</label>${html}`;return c;}
const spacingControl=makeControl('fgBoundarySpacing','Boundary spacing','<input id="fgBoundarySpacing" type="number" min="0.001" step="0.01" value="0.10" inputmode="decimal" />');
const alignControl=makeControl('fgBoundaryAlignment','Boundary alignment','<select id="fgBoundaryAlignment"><option value="surface" selected>Surface normal</option><option value="along">Along boundary</option><option value="across">Across boundary</option></select>');
const spacing=spacingControl.querySelector?.('#fgBoundarySpacing')||document.getElementById('fgBoundarySpacing');
const alignment=alignControl.querySelector?.('#fgBoundaryAlignment')||document.getElementById('fgBoundaryAlignment');

function section(name){return document.querySelector(`.mlr10-section[data-mlr10="${name}"]`)}
function body(name){const s=section(name);return s?.querySelector('.mlr10-body')||s}
function reveal(el){if(!el)return;for(const c of ['hidden','mlr24-hidden','mlr21-hidden','mlr20-hidden','mlr18-hidden','mlr17-hidden','mlr15-hidden','mlr14-hidden','mlr11-hidden','mlr10-hidden','mlr-context-hidden','mlr-mode-hidden','mlr-consolidated-hidden','mlr-wip6-hidden','muz-orient-hidden'])el.classList.remove(c);el.style.removeProperty('display')}
function hide(el){if(!el)return;el.style.setProperty('display','none','important')}
function activeMode(){const e=document.getElementById('replaceMode')||document.getElementById('mlrMode');const s=((e?.value||'')+' '+(e?.selectedOptions?.[0]?.textContent||'')).toLowerCase();if(/import|custom|instance object/.test(s))return'custom';if(/torus/.test(s))return'torus';return'other'}

function boundaryActive(){return placementSelect()?.value==='facegroupBoundary'}

 const pb=body('Placement'),ob=body('Orientation');if(pb){reveal(spacingControl);pb.appendChild(spacingControl)}if(ob){reveal(alignControl);ob.insertBefore(alignControl,ob.firstChild)}
 if(boundaryActive()){reveal(spacingControl);reveal(alignControl)}else{hide(spacingControl);hide(alignControl)}
 stamp();
}

function groupFromObject(obj,fallback){
 const candidates=[obj?.userData?.nomad?.group,obj?.geometry?.userData?.nomad?.group,obj?.userData?.extras?.nomad?.group,obj?.geometry?.userData?.extras?.nomad?.group,obj?.userData?.gltfExtensions?.nomad?.group,obj?.geometry?.userData?.gltfExtensions?.nomad?.group];
 for(const v of candidates)if(v!==undefined&&v!==null)return v;return fallback;
}
function qkey(p,t){return `${Math.round(p.x/t)},${Math.round(p.y/t)},${Math.round(p.z/t)}`}
function sourceBounds(){const b=new THREE.Box3();try{sourceRoot?.updateMatrixWorld?.(true);sourceRoot?.traverse?.(o=>{if(o.isMesh&&o.geometry?.attributes?.position)b.expandByObject(o)})}catch{}return b}
function collectGroupedTriangles(){
 if(!sourceRoot)return[];sourceRoot.updateMatrixWorld(true);const tris=[];let primitiveIndex=0;
 sourceRoot.traverse(obj=>{
   if(!obj.isMesh||!obj.geometry?.attributes?.position)return;const g=obj.geometry,p=g.getAttribute('position'),idx=g.index;const groups=g.groups?.length?g.groups:[{start:0,count:idx?idx.count:p.count}];
   for(let gi=0;gi<groups.length;gi++){
     const gr=groups[gi],fallback=glbPrimitiveGroups[primitiveIndex] ?? glbPrimitiveGroups[primitiveIndex+gi] ?? primitiveIndex+gi;const gid=groupFromObject(obj,fallback);
     const start=gr.start||0,end=Math.min(start+(gr.count||0),idx?idx.count:p.count);
     for(let i=start;i+2<end;i+=3){const ia=idx?idx.getX(i):i,ib=idx?idx.getX(i+1):i+1,ic=idx?idx.getX(i+2):i+2;const a=new THREE.Vector3(p.getX(ia),p.getY(ia),p.getZ(ia)).applyMatrix4(obj.matrixWorld),b=new THREE.Vector3(p.getX(ib),p.getY(ib),p.getZ(ib)).applyMatrix4(obj.matrixWorld),c=new THREE.Vector3(p.getX(ic),p.getY(ic),p.getZ(ic)).applyMatrix4(obj.matrixWorld);const n=b.clone().sub(a).cross(c.clone().sub(a));if(n.lengthSq()>1e-18)tris.push({a,b,c,normal:n.normalize(),group:gid});}
   }
   primitiveIndex+=Math.max(groups.length,1);
 });return tris;
}
function buildBoundarySegments(){
 const tris=collectGroupedTriangles();if(!tris.length)return[];const box=sourceBounds(),diag=Math.max(box.getSize(new THREE.Vector3()).length(),1e-6),tol=diag*1e-6;const edges=new Map();
 const add=(a,b,tri)=>{const ka=qkey(a,tol),kb=qkey(b,tol),key=ka<kb?`${ka}|${kb}`:`${kb}|${ka}`;let e=edges.get(key);if(!e){e={ka:ka<kb?ka:kb,kb:ka<kb?kb:ka,a:(ka<kb?a:b).clone(),b:(ka<kb?b:a).clone(),refs:[]};edges.set(key,e)}e.refs.push(tri)};
 for(const t of tris){add(t.a,t.b,t);add(t.b,t.c,t);add(t.c,t.a,t)}
 const centre=box.getCenter(new THREE.Vector3()),segments=[];const normalMode=activeMode()==='custom'?document.getElementById('customNormalMode')?.value:document.getElementById('torusNormalMode')?.value;
 for(const e of edges.values()){
   const groups=new Set(e.refs.map(r=>String(r.group)));if(groups.size<2)continue;
   let n=new THREE.Vector3(),seed=e.refs[0].normal.clone();for(const r of e.refs){const rn=r.normal.clone();if(rn.dot(seed)<0)rn.negate();n.add(rn)}if(n.lengthSq()<1e-14)continue;n.normalize();const mid=e.a.clone().add(e.b).multiplyScalar(.5),radial=mid.clone().sub(centre);if(normalMode==='outward'&&n.dot(radial)<0)n.negate();if(normalMode==='inward'&&n.dot(radial)>0)n.negate();segments.push({...e,normal:n});
 }
 return segments;
}
function traceBoundaryChains(segments){
 const adj=new Map();segments.forEach((s,i)=>{for(const k of [s.ka,s.kb]){let a=adj.get(k);if(!a){a=[];adj.set(k,a)}a.push(i)}});const used=new Uint8Array(segments.length),chains=[];
 function walk(startEdge,startKey){const points=[],normals=[];let ei=startEdge,key=startKey,guard=0;while(ei!=null&&!used[ei]&&guard++<segments.length+2){const s=segments[ei];used[ei]=1;const forward=s.ka===key,from=forward?s.a:s.b,to=forward?s.b:s.a,nextKey=forward?s.kb:s.ka;if(!points.length)points.push(from.clone());points.push(to.clone());normals.push(s.normal.clone());const next=(adj.get(nextKey)||[]).find(j=>!used[j]);if(next==null)break;key=nextKey;ei=next}return{points,normals,closed:points.length>2&&points[0].distanceToSquared(points[points.length-1])<1e-12}}
 for(const [key,list] of adj)if(list.length!==2)for(const ei of list)if(!used[ei])chains.push(walk(ei,key));for(let i=0;i<segments.length;i++)if(!used[i])chains.push(walk(i,segments[i].ka));return chains.filter(c=>c.points.length>1);
}
function sampleChain(chain,wanted){
 const pts=chain.points,segLen=[];let total=0;for(let i=0;i<pts.length-1;i++){const l=pts[i].distanceTo(pts[i+1]);segLen.push(l);total+=l}if(total<=1e-9)return[];const closed=chain.closed;let count=closed?Math.max(1,Math.round(total/wanted)):Math.max(2,Math.floor(total/wanted)+1);const step=closed?total/count:total/(count-1),out=[];
 for(let si=0;si<count;si++){let d=closed?si*step:Math.min(total,si*step),acc=0,idx=0;while(idx<segLen.length-1&&acc+segLen[idx]<d){acc+=segLen[idx];idx++}const l=Math.max(segLen[idx],1e-9),u=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(d-acc)/l)),p=pts[idx].clone().lerp(pts[idx+1],u),tangent=pts[idx+1].clone().sub(pts[idx]).normalize(),normal=(chain.normals[idx]||chain.normals[Math.max(0,idx-1)]||new THREE.Vector3(0,1,0)).clone().normalize();out.push({point:p,normal,tangent,localRadius:step*.5,isFacegroupBoundary:true,surfaceNormal:normal.clone(),boundaryTangent:tangent.clone()})}return out;
}
function buildFacegroupBoundaryPlacements(){const segs=buildBoundarySegments();if(!segs.length)return[];const wanted=Math.max(Number(spacing?.value||.1),.001),chains=traceBoundaryChains(segs),out=[];for(const c of chains)out.push(...sampleChain(c,wanted));return out;}

// Boundary-specific frame. The existing instance axis selector still determines which imported local axis maps to frame.normal.
const previousNodeFrame=torusNodeFrame;
torusNodeFrame=function(node){if(!node?.isFacegroupBoundary)return previousNodeFrame(node);const surface=node.surfaceNormal.clone().normalize(),t=node.boundaryTangent.clone().normalize();let target=surface.clone(),x=t.clone();if(alignment?.value==='along'){target.copy(t);x.copy(surface)}else if(alignment?.value==='across'){target.crossVectors(surface,t).normalize();x.copy(t)}x.addScaledVector(target,-x.dot(target));if(x.lengthSq()<1e-12){const h=Math.abs(target.y)<.9?new THREE.Vector3(0,1,0):new THREE.Vector3(1,0,0);x.crossVectors(h,target)}x.normalize();const y=new THREE.Vector3().crossVectors(target,x).normalize();x.copy(new THREE.Vector3().crossVectors(y,target).normalize());return{xAxis:x,yAxis:y,normal:target};};

// Preserve Normal Offset as a surface-normal offset even when the alignment target is tangent/across.
const previousTorusNodeData=torusInstanceDataForNode;
torusInstanceDataForNode=function(node){const d=previousTorusNodeData(node);if(!d||!node?.isFacegroupBoundary||alignment?.value==='surface')return d;const pos=new THREE.Vector3(),q=new THREE.Quaternion(),sc=new THREE.Vector3();d.matrix.decompose(pos,q,sc);const off=Number(document.getElementById('torusNormalOffset')?.value||0),frame=torusNodeFrame(node);pos.copy(node.point).addScaledVector(node.surfaceNormal,off);d.matrix.compose(pos,q,sc);return d;};
const previousCustomNodeMatrix=customPlacementMatrixForNode;
customPlacementMatrixForNode=function(node){const m=previousCustomNodeMatrix(node);if(!m||!node?.isFacegroupBoundary||alignment?.value==='surface')return m;const pos=new THREE.Vector3(),q=new THREE.Quaternion(),sc=new THREE.Vector3();m.decompose(pos,q,sc);const off=Number(document.getElementById('customNormalOffset')?.value||0);pos.copy(node.point).addScaledVector(node.surfaceNormal,off);return m.compose(pos,q,sc);};

async function withBoundaryPlacements(select,nativeGenerate){
 const originalValue=select.value,originalBuilder=buildSharedNodePlacements;select.value='node';buildSharedNodePlacements=()=>buildFacegroupBoundaryPlacements();
 try{const placements=buildFacegroupBoundaryPlacements();if(!placements.length){setStatus('No Nomad facegroup boundaries were detected in the source mesh');return;}setStatus(`Detected ${placements.length.toLocaleString()} evenly spaced facegroup-boundary placements…`);await nativeGenerate();setStatus(`Generated instances along Nomad facegroup boundaries • ${placements.length.toLocaleString()} placements • spacing ${Number(spacing.value).toFixed(3)}`)}finally{buildSharedNodePlacements=originalBuilder;select.value=originalValue;setTimeout(applyUI,0)}
}
const nativeTorusGenerate=generateFaceTorusResult;generateFaceTorusResult=async function(){const s=document.getElementById('torusPlacementMode');if(s?.value==='facegroupBoundary')return withBoundaryPlacements(s,nativeTorusGenerate);return nativeTorusGenerate();};
const nativeCustomGenerate=generateCustomInstanceResult;generateCustomInstanceResult=async function(){const s=document.getElementById('customPlacementMode');if(s?.value==='facegroupBoundary')return withBoundaryPlacements(s,nativeCustomGenerate);return nativeCustomGenerate();};

function stamp(){document.title=`${PRODUCT} v${VERSION}`;document.querySelectorAll('.version').forEach(x=>x.textContent=`v${VERSION}`);const av=document.querySelector('.about-version');if(av)av.textContent=`${PRODUCT} · Version ${VERSION}`;const title=document.querySelector('.title');if(title)title.textContent=PRODUCT;const bars=[...document.querySelectorAll('.mlr-statusbar,.mlr-wip8-statusbar')];bars.slice(1).forEach(x=>x.remove());if(bars[0]){const sp=bars[0].querySelectorAll('span');if(sp.length)sp[sp.length-1].textContent=`v${VERSION}`}}

})();



// MeshUtilz v0.9.1-wip.6 — boundary-only density + closed facegroup-boundary tube
(function(){
const PRODUCT='MeshUtilz', VERSION='0.9.1-wip.6';
if(typeof THREE==='undefined'||typeof sourceRoot==='undefined')return;

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 1. Facegroup-boundary density must remain one-dimensional along the seam.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
let boundaryDensityActive=false;
const nativeDensityRecords=typeof densityRecordsForFaces==='function'?densityRecordsForFaces:null;
if(nativeDensityRecords){
 densityRecordsForFaces=function(faces,placementMode,level){
   if(boundaryDensityActive&&placementMode==='node'){
     return buildSharedNodePlacements(faces).map(p=>({kind:'node',source:p}));
   }
   return nativeDensityRecords(faces,placementMode,level);
 };
}
function boundarySelected(select){return select?.value==='facegroupBoundary'}
function densityInputFor(select){return select?.id==='customPlacementMode'?document.getElementById('customDensity'):document.getElementById('torusDensity')}
function effectiveBoundarySpacing(select){
 const base=document.getElementById('fgBoundarySpacing');
 const d=densityInputFor(select);const baseSpacing=Math.max(Number(base?.value||0.1),0.000001);const level=Number(d?.value||0);
 // Each two slider steps approximately doubles/halves density while keeping every sample on the seam.
 return baseSpacing/Math.pow(2,level/2);
}
function wrapBoundaryDensity(fn,selectId){
 if(typeof fn!=='function')return fn;
 return async function(){
   const select=document.getElementById(selectId);if(!boundarySelected(select))return fn.apply(this,arguments);
   const spacing=document.getElementById('fgBoundarySpacing');const old=spacing?.value;
   if(spacing)spacing.value=String(effectiveBoundarySpacing(select));boundaryDensityActive=true;
   try{return await fn.apply(this,arguments)}finally{boundaryDensityActive=false;if(spacing&&old!=null)spacing.value=old;setTimeout(refreshBoundaryDensityUI,0)}
 };
}
if(typeof generateFaceTorusResult==='function')generateFaceTorusResult=wrapBoundaryDensity(generateFaceTorusResult,'torusPlacementMode');
if(typeof generateCustomInstanceResult==='function')generateCustomInstanceResult=wrapBoundaryDensity(generateCustomInstanceResult,'customPlacementMode');

function refreshBoundaryDensityUI(){
 for(const select of [document.getElementById('torusPlacementMode'),document.getElementById('customPlacementMode')]){
   if(!select)continue;const density=densityInputFor(select),out=density?document.getElementById(density.id+'Out'):null;
   if(boundarySelected(select)&&density&&out){const eff=effectiveBoundarySpacing(select);out.value=`${Number(density.value)>0?'+':''}${density.value} · ${eff.toFixed(3)}`;}
 }
 stamp();
}
for(const id of ['torusPlacementMode','customPlacementMode','torusDensity','customDensity','fgBoundarySpacing']){
 const e=document.getElementById(id);e?.addEventListener(e?.type==='range'?'input':'input',refreshBoundaryDensityUI);e?.addEventListener('change',refreshBoundaryDensityUI);
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 2. Independent Nomad facegroup seam reader for Boundary Tube mode.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
let tubeGlbPrimitiveGroups=[];
function readPrimitiveGroups(file){
 if(!file||!file.name?.toLowerCase().endsWith('.glb')){tubeGlbPrimitiveGroups=[];return;}
 file.arrayBuffer().then(buf=>{try{
   const dv=new DataView(buf);if(dv.getUint32(0,true)!==0x46546c67)return;let off=12,json=null;
   while(off+8<=buf.byteLength){const len=dv.getUint32(off,true),type=dv.getUint32(off+4,true);off+=8;if(type===0x4e4f534a){json=JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(new Uint8Array(buf,off,len)).replace(/\0+$/,''));break;}off+=len;}
   const groups=[];for(const mesh of json?.meshes||[])for(const prim of mesh.primitives||[])groups.push(prim?.extras?.nomad?.group??null);tubeGlbPrimitiveGroups=groups;
 }catch(e){console.warn('MeshUtilz boundary-tube GLB metadata parse failed',e);tubeGlbPrimitiveGroups=[];}});
}
try{fileInput?.addEventListener('change',()=>{const f=fileInput.files?.[0];if(f)readPrimitiveGroups(f)},true)}catch{}

function qKey(p,t){return `${Math.round(p.x/t)},${Math.round(p.y/t)},${Math.round(p.z/t)}`}
function objectGroup(obj,fallback){
 const vals=[obj?.userData?.nomad?.group,obj?.geometry?.userData?.nomad?.group,obj?.userData?.extras?.nomad?.group,obj?.geometry?.userData?.extras?.nomad?.group];
 for(const v of vals)if(v!==undefined&&v!==null)return v;return fallback;
}
function collectBoundaryTubeTriangles(){
 if(!sourceRoot)return[];sourceRoot.updateMatrixWorld(true);const tris=[];let primitiveIndex=0;
 sourceRoot.traverse(obj=>{
   if(!obj.isMesh||!obj.geometry?.attributes?.position)return;
   const g=obj.geometry,p=g.getAttribute('position'),idx=g.index;const groups=g.groups?.length?g.groups:[{start:0,count:idx?idx.count:p.count}];
   for(let gi=0;gi<groups.length;gi++){
     const gr=groups[gi],fallback=tubeGlbPrimitiveGroups[primitiveIndex+gi]??primitiveIndex+gi,gid=objectGroup(obj,fallback),start=gr.start||0,end=Math.min(start+(gr.count||0),idx?idx.count:p.count);
     for(let i=start;i+2<end;i+=3){const ids=[idx?idx.getX(i):i,idx?idx.getX(i+1):i+1,idx?idx.getX(i+2):i+2];const v=ids.map(k=>new THREE.Vector3(p.getX(k),p.getY(k),p.getZ(k)).applyMatrix4(obj.matrixWorld));const n=v[1].clone().sub(v[0]).cross(v[2].clone().sub(v[0]));if(n.lengthSq()>1e-18)tris.push({v,normal:n.normalize(),group:gid});}
   }
   primitiveIndex+=Math.max(groups.length,1);
 });return tris;
}
function boundaryTubeSegments(){
 const tris=collectBoundaryTubeTriangles();if(!tris.length)return[];const box=new THREE.Box3();tris.forEach(t=>t.v.forEach(p=>box.expandByPoint(p)));const tol=Math.max(box.getSize(new THREE.Vector3()).length(),1e-6)*1e-6,edges=new Map();
 const add=(a,b,t)=>{const ka=qKey(a,tol),kb=qKey(b,tol),key=ka<kb?`${ka}|${kb}`:`${kb}|${ka}`;let e=edges.get(key);if(!e){const forward=ka<kb;e={ka:forward?ka:kb,kb:forward?kb:ka,a:(forward?a:b).clone(),b:(forward?b:a).clone(),refs:[]};edges.set(key,e)}e.refs.push(t)};
 for(const t of tris){add(t.v[0],t.v[1],t);add(t.v[1],t.v[2],t);add(t.v[2],t.v[0],t)}
 const out=[];for(const e of edges.values()){if(new Set(e.refs.map(r=>String(r.group))).size<2)continue;let n=new THREE.Vector3(),seed=e.refs[0].normal;for(const r of e.refs){const rn=r.normal.clone();if(rn.dot(seed)<0)rn.negate();n.add(rn)}if(n.lengthSq()>1e-14)out.push({...e,normal:n.normalize()});}return out;
}
function traceTubeChains(segments){
 const adj=new Map();segments.forEach((s,i)=>{for(const k of [s.ka,s.kb]){let a=adj.get(k);if(!a){a=[];adj.set(k,a)}a.push(i)}});const used=new Uint8Array(segments.length),chains=[];
 function walk(seed,key){const pts=[];let ei=seed,guard=0;while(ei!=null&&!used[ei]&&guard++<segments.length+3){const s=segments[ei];used[ei]=1;const f=s.ka===key,a=f?s.a:s.b,b=f?s.b:s.a,nextKey=f?s.kb:s.ka;if(!pts.length)pts.push(a.clone());pts.push(b.clone());const candidates=(adj.get(nextKey)||[]).filter(j=>!used[j]);if(!candidates.length)break;ei=candidates[0];key=nextKey;}const closed=pts.length>2&&pts[0].distanceToSquared(pts[pts.length-1])<1e-10;if(closed)pts.pop();return{points:pts,closed};}
 for(const [key,list] of adj)if(list.length!==2)for(const ei of list)if(!used[ei])chains.push(walk(ei,key));for(let i=0;i<segments.length;i++)if(!used[i])chains.push(walk(i,segments[i].ka));return chains.filter(c=>c.points.length>=2);
}

// Exact piecewise-linear curve: TubeGeometry follows the detected seam rather than smoothing away from it.
class BoundaryPolylineCurve extends THREE.Curve{
 constructor(points,closed){super();this.points=points.map(p=>p.clone());this.closed=closed;this.lengths=[];this.total=0;const count=closed?this.points.length:this.points.length-1;for(let i=0;i<count;i++){const l=this.points[i].distanceTo(this.points[(i+1)%this.points.length]);this.lengths.push(l);this.total+=l;}}
 getPoint(t,target=new THREE.Vector3()){if(!this.points.length)return target.set(0,0,0);if(this.total<=1e-12)return target.copy(this.points[0]);let d=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,t))*this.total,acc=0;for(let i=0;i<this.lengths.length;i++){const l=this.lengths[i];if(d<=acc+l||i===this.lengths.length-1){const u=l>1e-12?(d-acc)/l:0;return target.copy(this.points[i]).lerp(this.points[(i+1)%this.points.length],Math.max(0,Math.min(1,u)));}acc+=l;}return target.copy(this.points[this.points.length-1]);}
}

Closed boundaries generate closed watertight tubes.</p></div>`;panel.appendChild(tubeSection)}

function applyTubeUI(){if(!tubeSection)return;const on=activeNativeMode()==='facegroupBoundaryTube';tubeSection.style.display=on?'':'none';if(on){tubeSection.classList.remove('hidden','mlr24-hidden','mlr-mode-hidden');tubeSection.open=true;}stamp()}

function offsetChain(points,offset){if(Math.abs(offset)<1e-12)return points.map(p=>p.clone());const centre=new THREE.Box3().setFromPoints(points).getCenter(new THREE.Vector3());return points.map(p=>{const n=p.clone().sub(centre);if(n.lengthSq()<1e-12)n.set(0,1,0);return p.clone().addScaledVector(n.normalize(),offset)});}
async function generateBoundaryTube(){
 if(!sourceRoot)return;const segments=boundaryTubeSegments(),chains=traceTubeChains(segments);if(!chains.length){setStatus('No Nomad facegroup boundaries were detected for tube generation');return;}
 const dia=Number(document.getElementById('fgTubeDiameter')?.value||0.05),radius=dia*0.5,rad=Math.max(3,Number(radial?.value||12)),mult=Math.max(1,Number(pathSeg?.value||2)),off=Number(document.getElementById('fgTubeNormalOffset')?.value||0);if(!Number.isFinite(radius)||radius<=0){setStatus('Tube diameter must be greater than zero');return;}
 generateBtn.disabled=true;try{await new Promise(r=>requestAnimationFrame(r));disposeObject(resultRoot);resultRoot=new THREE.Group();resultRoot.name='FacegroupBoundaryTubes';let made=0,totalFaces=0,closedCount=0;
   for(const chain of chains){if(chain.points.length<2)continue;const pts=offsetChain(chain.points,off),curve=new BoundaryPolylineCurve(pts,chain.closed),pathCount=Math.max(chain.closed?pts.length*mult:Math.max(1,(pts.length-1)*mult),3);const g=new THREE.TubeGeometry(curve,pathCount,radius,rad,chain.closed);g.name='FacegroupBoundaryTubeGeometry';g.computeVertexNormals();const mat=new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({color:0xe6a4ff,roughness:0.52,metalness:0.04,side:THREE.DoubleSide});const mesh=new THREE.Mesh(g,mat);mesh.name=chain.closed?'ClosedFacegroupBoundaryTube':'OpenFacegroupBoundaryTube';resultRoot.add(mesh);made++;if(chain.closed)closedCount++;totalFaces+=g.index?g.index.count/3:g.getAttribute('position').count/3;}
   scene.add(resultRoot);sourceRoot.visible=showSource.checked;exportBtn.disabled=false;setStatus(`Generated ${made} facegroup-boundary tube${made===1?'':'s'} • ${closedCount} closed • diameter ${dia.toFixed(3)}`);setOutputMetrics({faces:Math.round(totalFaces),holes:closedCount,exported:false});
 }catch(e){console.error(e);setStatus(`Boundary tube generation failed: ${e.message||e}`)}finally{generateBtn.disabled=false;stamp()}
}

// MeshUtilz v0.9.2-wip.59 — consolidated primitive importer, bridge, transform capture, and preview
(function(){
const VERSION='0.9.2-wip.59',PRODUCT='MeshUtilz';let live=null,previewRoot=null,previewGeo=null;
function stamp(){document.title=PRODUCT+' v'+VERSION;document.querySelectorAll('.version').forEach(x=>x.textContent='v'+VERSION);const a=document.querySelector('.about-version');if(a)a.textContent=PRODUCT+' · Version '+VERSION;const bars=[...document.querySelectorAll('.mlr-statusbar,.mlr-wip8-statusbar')];bars.slice(1).forEach(x=>x.remove());if(bars[0]){const s=bars[0].querySelectorAll('span');if(s.length)s[s.length-1].textContent='v'+VERSION}}
function u64(dv,o){return Number(dv.getBigUint64(o,true))}
function parse(bytes){if(new TextDecoder().decode(bytes.slice(0,12))!=='Nomad Sculpt')throw Error('Not a Nomad Sculpt project');if(bytes.length<64)throw Error('NOM container is truncated');const dv=new DataView(bytes.buffer,bytes.byteOffset,bytes.byteLength),jo=u64(dv,24),jl=u64(dv,32),bo=u64(dv,40),bl=u64(dv,48);if(jo<64||jo+jl>bytes.length||bo<jo+jl||bo+bl>bytes.length)throw Error('Invalid NOM container offsets');return{bytes,jo,jl,bo,bl,json:JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(bytes.slice(jo,jo+jl))),bin:bytes.slice(bo,bo+bl)}}
function kindOf(m){const mt=String(m?.mesh_type||'').toLowerCase(),keys=Object.keys(m||{}).join(' ').toLowerCase();if(mt==='tube'||m?.config_tube)return'TUBE';if(mt==='torus'||/config[_-]?torus|\btorus\b/.test(keys))return'TORUS';if(mt==='box'||m?.config_box)return'BOX';if(mt==='cylinder'||m?.config_cylinder)return'CYLINDER';return null}
function findProcedural(p){function walk(nodes){for(const n of nodes||[]){if(Number.isInteger(n.mesh)){const m=p.json.meshes?.[n.mesh],kind=kindOf(m);if(kind)return{node:n,mesh:m,index:n.mesh,kind}}const q=walk(n.children);if(q)return q}return null}return walk(p.json.scene)}
function lz4Block(src,expected){if(!src?.length)return new Uint8Array();let si=0;if(src.length>=8){const dv=new DataView(src.buffer,src.byteOffset,src.byteLength),raw=dv.getUint32(0,true),comp=dv.getUint32(4,true);if(raw===expected&&comp<=src.length-8)si=8}const out=new Uint8Array(expected);let di=0;while(si<src.length&&di<expected){const token=src[si++];let lit=token>>4;if(lit===15){let x=255;while(x===255&&si<src.length){x=src[si++];lit+=x}}if(si+lit>src.length)lit=Math.max(0,src.length-si);out.set(src.subarray(si,si+lit),di);si+=lit;di+=lit;if(si>=src.length||di>=expected)break;if(si+2>src.length)break;const off=src[si]|(src[si+1]<<8);si+=2;if(!off||off>di)throw Error('Invalid LZ4 block');let ml=(token&15)+4;if((token&15)===15){let x=255;while(x===255&&si<src.length){x=src[si++];ml+=x}}for(let k=0;k<ml&&di<expected;k++,di++)out[di]=out[di-off]}if(di!==expected)throw Error('Incomplete LZ4 cache');return out}
function rawField(obj,a,size){if(!a)return null;const src=obj.parsed.bin.slice(a.offset,a.offset+a.length),expected=a.count*size;return a.lz4?lz4Block(src,expected):src}
function makeGeometry(obj){const m=obj.mesh,g=new THREE.BufferGeometry(),vb=rawField(obj,m.vertices,12);if(!vb?.length)throw Error('Live NOM object has no cached vertex geometry');const vf=new Float32Array(vb.buffer,vb.byteOffset,Math.floor(vb.byteLength/4));g.setAttribute('position',new THREE.BufferAttribute(new Float32Array(vf),3));const uvb=rawField(obj,m.uvs,8);if(uvb?.length){const u=new Float32Array(uvb.buffer,uvb.byteOffset,Math.floor(uvb.byteLength/4));g.setAttribute('uv',new THREE.BufferAttribute(new Float32Array(u),2))}const fb=rawField(obj,m.faces,16);if(fb?.length){const f=new Int32Array(fb.buffer,fb.byteOffset,Math.floor(fb.byteLength/4)),idx=[];for(let i=0;i+3<f.length;i+=4){const a=f[i],b=f[i+1],c=f[i+2],d=f[i+3];if(a<0||b<0||c<0)continue;idx.push(a,b,c);if(d>=0&&d!==c)idx.push(a,c,d)}g.setIndex(idx)}g.computeVertexNormals();g.computeBoundingBox();g.computeBoundingSphere();return g}
function clearPreview(){try{const stale=[];scene?.traverse?.(o=>{if(o?.name==='MeshUtilz Live NOM Preview')stale.push(o)});for(const o of stale)o.parent?.remove?.(o)}catch{}if(previewRoot)previewRoot.traverse?.(o=>o.material?.dispose?.());previewRoot=null}
function previewTransforms(){const a=window.__muLiveAuthoritativeTransforms;return Array.isArray(a)?a:[]}
function rebuildPreview(){clearPreview();if(!live)return 0;try{if(!previewGeo)previewGeo=makeGeometry(live);const material=new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({color:0x77b7ff,roughness:.58,metalness:.04,side:THREE.DoubleSide,transparent:true,opacity:.88}),root=new THREE.Group();root.name='MeshUtilz Live NOM Preview';const pp=previewTransforms();if(pp.length){for(const t of pp){const o=new THREE.Mesh(previewGeo,material);if(Array.isArray(t.matrix)&&t.matrix.length===16)o.matrix.fromArray(t.matrix).decompose(o.position,o.quaternion,o.scale);else{const p=t.position||t.p,q=t.quaternion||t.q,s=t.scale||t.s;if(p)o.position.set(p.x,p.y,p.z);if(q)o.quaternion.set(q.x,q.y,q.z,q.w);if(s)o.scale.set(s.x,s.y,s.z)}root.add(o)}}else{const o=new THREE.Mesh(previewGeo,material);o.name='Live NOM Master Preview';root.add(o)}previewRoot=root;scene?.add?.(root);info();return pp.length||1}catch(e){setStatus?.('Live NOM preview unavailable: '+(e.message||e));return 0}}
function resetPreviewGeometry(){clearPreview();previewGeo?.dispose?.();previewGeo=null}

function capture(){try{if(typeof resultRoot==='undefined'||!resultRoot)return 0;resultRoot.updateMatrixWorld?.(true);const sets=[];resultRoot.traverse?.(o=>{if(o?.isInstancedMesh&&o.count>0)sets.push(o)});if(!sets.length)return 0;sets.sort((a,b)=>b.count-a.count);const src=sets[0],m=new THREE.Matrix4(),wm=new THREE.Matrix4(),p=new THREE.Vector3(),q=new THREE.Quaternion(),s=new THREE.Vector3(),out=[];for(let i=0;i<src.count;i++){src.getMatrixAt(i,m);wm.multiplyMatrices(src.matrixWorld||new THREE.Matrix4(),m);wm.decompose(p,q,s);if(Math.abs(s.x)<1e-10||Math.abs(s.y)<1e-10||Math.abs(s.z)<1e-10)continue;out.push({position:p.clone(),quaternion:q.clone(),scale:s.clone(),matrix:wm.toArray()})}window.__muLiveAuthoritativeTransforms=out;window.__muLivePreviewPlacements=out;window.lastPlacements=out;rebuildPreview();syncInstanceSizeUI();syncProceduralContextUI();info();if(out.length)setStep('download');return out.length}catch(e){console.warn('WIP59 transform capture failed',e);return 0}}
function loadPrimitiveBytes(bytes,name,internalFactory=false){window.__muLiveAuthoritativeTransforms=[];resetPreviewGeometry();const p=parse(bytes instanceof Uint8Array?bytes:new Uint8Array(bytes)),x=findProcedural(p);if(!x)throw Error('WIP59 supports procedural Tube, Torus, Box, or Cylinder NOM objects');live={parsed:p,...x,name:name||('MeshUtilz '+x.kind),internalFactory:!!internalFactory};window.__muLiveNomObject=live;previewGeo=makeGeometry(live);const useBtn=document.getElementById('muzUseLiveNom');if(useBtn)useBtn.disabled=false;rebuildPreview();info();setStep('assign');if(typeof window.dispatchEvent==='function'&&typeof CustomEvent==='function')window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('mu:primitive-loaded',{detail:{kind:x.kind,internalFactory:!!internalFactory}}));setStatus?.('WIP59: '+(internalFactory?'internal ':'')+'procedural '+x.kind+' loaded');stamp();return live}
function rejectLoad(error){live=null;window.__muLiveNomObject=null;const useBtn=document.getElementById('muzUseLiveNom');if(useBtn)useBtn.disabled=true;setStep('load');const el=document.getElementById('muzLiveNomInfo');if(el)el.textContent='NOM import failed: '+(error.message||error);setStatus?.('NOM import failed: '+(error.message||error))}
