// MeshUtilz v0.9.2-wip.85 — deterministic Hair / Fur clumping
(function(){
'use strict';
if(typeof THREE==='undefined'||!window.__muW83Hair)return;
const VERSION='0.9.2-wip.85',WORKFLOW='nomad-hair-fur';let applying=false;
function active(){return window.__muWorkflowMode===WORKFLOW||document.body?.classList.contains('muz83-hair')}
function n(id){return Number(document.getElementById(id)?.value)}
function settings(){const out={count:Math.round(n('muzW85ClumpCount')),radius:n('muzW85ClumpRadius'),strength:n('muzW85ClumpStrength')/100,seed:Math.trunc(n('muzW83Seed'))};if(!Number.isInteger(out.count)||out.count<1||out.count>128)throw Error('Clump count must be 1–128');if(!Number.isFinite(out.radius)||out.radius<=0)throw Error('Clump radius must be positive');if(!Number.isFinite(out.strength)||out.strength<0||out.strength>1)throw Error('Clump strength must be 0–100%');return out}
function rng(seed){let a=(seed|0)>>>0;return()=>{a=(a+0x6d2b79f5)>>>0;let t=a;t=Math.imul(t^(t>>>15),t|1);t^=t+Math.imul(t^(t>>>7),t|61);return((t^(t>>>14))>>>0)/4294967296}}
function signature(records){let h=2166136261>>>0;for(const r of records)for(const v of[...r.root,...r.normal,r.length,r.width,...r.matrix]){const t=Number(v).toFixed(6);for(let i=0;i<t.length;i++){h^=t.charCodeAt(i);h=Math.imul(h,16777619)}}return(h>>>0).toString(16).padStart(8,'0')}
function direction(record,centres,s){const base=new THREE.Vector3().fromArray(record.direction||record.normal).normalize();if(!s.strength)return base;const root=new THREE.Vector3().fromArray(record.root);let best=null,d2=Infinity;for(const c of centres){const d=root.distanceToSquared(c);if(d<d2){d2=d;best=c}}if(!best||d2<1e-12)return base;const pull=best.clone().sub(root);if(pull.length()>s.radius)pull.setLength(s.radius);const target=base.clone().addScaledVector(pull,1/Math.max(s.radius,1e-9)).normalize();return base.lerp(target,s.strength).normalize()}
function sync(mats){if(typeof resultRoot!=='undefined'&&resultRoot){const m=new THREE.Matrix4();resultRoot.traverse?.(o=>{if(!o?.isInstancedMesh||o.count!==mats.length)return;for(let i=0;i<mats.length;i++){m.fromArray(mats[i].matrix);o.setMatrixAt(i,m)}o.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate=true})}window.__muWIP59Support?.rebuildPreview?.()}
function apply(){if(applying||!active())return;const state=window.__muW83Hair.state,mats=window.__muLiveAuthoritativeTransforms;if(!state?.records?.length||mats?.length!==state.records.length)return;applying=true;try{const s=settings(),random=rng(s.seed+8501),centres=[];for(let i=0;i<Math.min(s.count,state.records.length);i++)centres.push(new THREE.Vector3().fromArray(state.records[Math.floor(random()*state.records.length)].root));const q=new THREE.Quaternion(),m=new THREE.Matrix4(),p=new THREE.Vector3(),scale=new THREE.Vector3();for(let i=0;i<state.records.length;i++){const r=state.records[i],normal=new THREE.Vector3().fromArray(r.normal),dir=direction(r,centres,s),root=new THREE.Vector3().fromArray(r.root);q.setFromUnitVectors(new THREE.Vector3(0,1,0),dir);p.copy(root).addScaledVector(normal,state.settings.offset).addScaledVector(dir,r.length*.5);scale.set(r.width,r.length/state.baseLength,r.width);m.compose(p,q,scale);const matrix=m.toArray();r.clumpDirection=dir.toArray();r.matrix=matrix.slice();mats[i]={position:p.clone(),quaternion:q.clone(),scale:scale.clone(),matrix}}state.clump=s;state.signature=signature(state.records);window.__muLiveAuthoritativeTransforms=mats;window.__muLivePreviewPlacements=mats;window.lastPlacements=mats;sync(mats)}finally{applying=false}}

function row(id,label,html){let e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)return control(id);const wrap=document.createElement('div');wrap.className='control muz80-orient';wrap.innerHTML='<label for="'+id+'">'+label+'</label>'+html;body()?.appendChild(wrap);return wrap}
function ensure(prefix){
 const field=document.getElementById(prefix+'OrientationField');if(field){const label=control(prefix+'OrientationField')?.querySelector('label');if(label)label.textContent='Surface direction';for(const option of field.options){if(option.value==='local')option.textContent='Local face normal';if(option.value==='smooth')option.textContent='Neighbour-smoothed normal'}}
 row(prefix+'RollMode','Roll alignment','<select id="'+prefix+'RollMode"><option value="legacy" selected>Legacy surface direction</option><option value="shared">Shared world direction</option></select>');

 row(prefix+'RollOffset','Roll offset','<output id="'+prefix+'RollOffsetOut">0°</output><input id="'+prefix+'RollOffset" type="range" min="-180" max="180" value="0" step="1" />');
 row(prefix+'RandomRoll','Random roll','<input id="'+prefix+'RandomRoll" type="checkbox" />');
 row(prefix+'RandomSpread','Random variation','<output id="'+prefix+'RandomSpreadOut">0°</output><input id="'+prefix+'RandomSpread" type="range" min="0" max="180" value="0" step="1" />');
 row(prefix+'RandomSeed','Orientation seed','<input id="'+prefix+'RandomSeed" type="number" inputmode="numeric" value="80" step="1" />');

}
function guide(prefix){const value=document.getElementById(prefix+'SharedGuide')?.value;return value==='y'?new THREE.Vector3(0,1,0):value==='z'?new THREE.Vector3(0,0,1):new THREE.Vector3(1,0,0)}
function hash(point,seed){let h=2166136261>>>0;const values=[Math.round((point?.x||0)*1e5),Math.round((point?.y||0)*1e5),Math.round((point?.z||0)*1e5),Number(seed)||0];for(const value of values){const v=value|0;for(let i=0;i<4;i++){h^=(v>>>(i*8))&255;h=Math.imul(h,16777619)}}h^=h>>>16;h=Math.imul(h,0x7feb352d);h^=h>>>15;return(h>>>0)/4294967296}
function rotateRoll(frame,angle){if(Math.abs(angle)<1e-12)return frame;const n=frame.normal.clone().normalize(),x=frame.xAxis.clone().normalize(),y=new THREE.Vector3().crossVectors(n,x).normalize(),c=Math.cos(angle),s=Math.sin(angle);x.multiplyScalar(c).addScaledVector(y,s).normalize();return{xAxis:x,yAxis:new THREE.Vector3().crossVectors(n,x).normalize(),normal:n,centre:frame.centre}}
function sharedFrame(base,prefix){const n=base.normal.clone().normalize(),g=guide(prefix),x=g.clone().addScaledVector(n,-g.dot(n));if(x.lengthSq()<1e-10){diagnostics.fallbackFrames++;const candidates=[new THREE.Vector3(1,0,0),new THREE.Vector3(0,1,0),new THREE.Vector3(0,0,1)].sort((a,b)=>Math.abs(a.dot(n))-Math.abs(b.dot(n)));x.copy(candidates[0]).addScaledVector(n,-candidates[0].dot(n))}x.normalize();const y=new THREE.Vector3().crossVectors(n,x).normalize();x.copy(new THREE.Vector3().crossVectors(y,n).normalize());return{xAxis:x,yAxis:y,normal:n,centre:base.centre}}
function pointKey(point){return[Math.round((point?.x||0)*1e5),Math.round((point?.y||0)*1e5),Math.round((point?.z||0)*1e5)].join(',')}
function rollSignature(){let h=2166136261>>>0;for(const entry of[...rollAngles].sort((a,b)=>a[0].localeCompare(b[0]))){const text=entry[0]+':'+Number(entry[1]).toFixed(6);for(let i=0;i<text.length;i++){h^=text.charCodeAt(i);h=Math.imul(h,16777619)}}return(h>>>0).toString(16).padStart(8,'0')}
function orient(base,point){if(!base)return base;const prefix=activePrefix();if(!prefix||networkActive())return base;diagnostics.frames++;const mode=document.getElementById(prefix+'RollMode')?.value||'legacy';diagnostics.lastMode=mode;let frame=mode==='shared'?sharedFrame(base,prefix):base;if(mode==='shared')diagnostics.sharedFrames++;let degrees=Number(document.getElementById(prefix+'RollOffset')?.value||0),random=document.getElementById(prefix+'RandomRoll')?.checked,spread=Number(document.getElementById(prefix+'RandomSpread')?.value||0);if(random&&spread>0){degrees+=(hash(point,document.getElementById(prefix+'RandomSeed')?.value)*2-1)*spread;diagnostics.randomisedFrames++}rollAngles.set(pointKey(point),degrees);frame=rotateRoll(frame,THREE.MathUtils.degToRad(degrees));const determinant=new THREE.Matrix4().makeBasis(frame.xAxis,frame.yAxis,frame.normal).determinant();if(!Number.isFinite(determinant)||determinant<0.999||determinant>1.001)diagnostics.invalidFrames++;return frame}
torusLocalFrame=function(face,normal,centre){return orient(nativeFaceFrame(face,normal,centre),centre)};
torusNodeFrame=function(node){return orient(nativeNodeFrame(node),node?.point)};
function applyUI(){ensure('torus');ensure('custom');const prefix=activePrefix(),s=section();for(const p of['torus','custom'])for(const el of document.querySelectorAll('[data-muz80-prefix="'+p+'"]'))(p===prefix&&!networkActive()?show:hide)(el);if(!prefix||networkActive()){for(const p of['torus','custom'])for(const id of[p+'RollMode',p+'SharedGuide',p+'RollOffset',p+'RandomRoll',p+'RandomSpread',p+'RandomSeed',p+'RollDiagnostic'])hide(control(id));return}show(s);s.open=true;const target=body(),mode=document.getElementById(prefix+'RollMode')?.value||'legacy',random=document.getElementById(prefix+'RandomRoll')?.checked;for(const id of[prefix+'OrientationField',prefix+'OrientationSmooth',prefix+'RollMode',prefix+'SharedGuide',prefix+'RollOffset',prefix+'RandomRoll',prefix+'RandomSpread',prefix+'RandomSeed',prefix+'RollDiagnostic',prefix+'Axis',prefix+'TiltX',prefix+'TiltY']){const c=control(id);if(c){show(c);target?.appendChild(c)}}if(mode!=='shared')hide(control(prefix+'SharedGuide'));if(!random){hide(control(prefix+'RandomSpread'));hide(control(prefix+'RandomSeed'))}const field=document.getElementById(prefix+'OrientationField');if(field?.value!=='smooth')hide(control(prefix+'OrientationSmooth'));const note=document.getElementById(prefix+'RollDiagnostic'),surface=field?.value==='smooth'?'Neighbour-smoothed normal':'Local face normal',roll=mode==='shared'?'shared World '+String(document.getElementById(prefix+'SharedGuide')?.value||'x').toUpperCase():'legacy surface roll',randomText=random&&Number(document.getElementById(prefix+'RandomSpread')?.value||0)>0?' · random ±'+document.getElementById(prefix+'RandomSpread').value+'° seed '+document.getElementById(prefix+'RandomSeed').value:'';if(note){note.textContent=surface+' · '+roll+randomText+(diagnostics.frames?' · '+diagnostics.frames+' frames · '+diagnostics.fallbackFrames+' fallbacks · '+diagnostics.invalidFrames+' invalid':'');note.dataset.frames=String(diagnostics.frames);note.dataset.shared=String(diagnostics.sharedFrames);note.dataset.fallbacks=String(diagnostics.fallbackFrames);note.dataset.randomised=String(diagnostics.randomisedFrames);note.dataset.invalid=String(diagnostics.invalidFrames);note.dataset.signature=rollSignature()}}
function resetDiagnostics(){Object.assign(diagnostics,{frames:0,sharedFrames:0,fallbackFrames:0,randomisedFrames:0,invalidFrames:0});rollAngles.clear()}

window.__muW83Orientation={version:VERSION,diagnostics,applyUI,describe(){const p=activePrefix();return{active:p,networkBypassed:networkActive(),surface:document.getElementById(p+'OrientationField')?.value||null,roll:document.getElementById(p+'RollMode')?.value||null,guide:document.getElementById(p+'SharedGuide')?.value||null,random:!!document.getElementById(p+'RandomRoll')?.checked,spread:Number(document.getElementById(p+'RandomSpread')?.value||0),seed:Number(document.getElementById(p+'RandomSeed')?.value||0),diagnostics:{...diagnostics}}}};
})();

// MeshUtilz v0.9.2 RC2.2.1 — six-sided Tube cache validation fix
(function(){
'use strict';
const authority=window.__muPrimitiveParameterAuthority,adapters=window.__muPrimitiveParameterAdapters;
if(!authority||!adapters?.TUBE)return;
const previous=authority.validateLive;
function close(a,b,t=3e-3){return Math.abs(a-b)<=t*Math.max(1,Math.abs(a),Math.abs(b))}
function tubeBounds(live){const field=live.mesh.vertices,dv=new DataView(live.parsed.bin.buffer,live.parsed.bin.byteOffset,live.parsed.bin.byteLength);let minY=Infinity,maxY=-Infinity,maxRadius=0;for(let i=0;i<field.count;i++){const at=field.offset+i*12,x=dv.getFloat32(at,true),y=dv.getFloat32(at+4,true),z=dv.getFloat32(at+8,true);minY=Math.min(minY,y);maxY=Math.max(maxY,y);maxRadius=Math.max(maxRadius,Math.hypot(x,z))}return{length:maxY-minY,radius:maxRadius}}
function validateLive(live){if(live?.kind!=='TUBE'||!live.internalFactory)return previous?.(live);const state=live.parameterState;if(!state?.fields?.length)return true;if(live.mesh.vertices?.lz4)throw Error('WIP59 validation failed: edited compressed/LZ4 Tube cache');const actual=adapters.TUBE.read(live.mesh.config_tube);for(const [key] of state.fields)if(!close(Number(actual[key]),Number(state.values[key]),1e-6))throw Error('WIP59 validation failed: '+key+' does not match the parameter panel');const bounds=tubeBounds(live),values=state.values;if(!close(bounds.length,values.length)||!close(bounds.radius,values.radius))throw Error('WIP59 validation failed: Tube cache length or radial extent does not match its Nomad dimensions');return true}
authority.validateLive=validateLive;window.__muRC221TubeValidation={version:'0.9.2 RC2.2.1',validateLive,tubeBounds};
function brand(){document.title='MeshUtilz v0.9.2 RC2.2.1';document.body?.setAttribute('data-meshutilz-release','rc221');document.querySelectorAll('.version').forEach(e=>e.textContent='v0.9.2 RC2.2.1');const about=document.querySelector('.about-version');if(about)about.textContent='MeshUtilz · Version 0.9.2 RC2.2.1'}

})();

const PRESETS={'4x6':{x:4,y:6,file:'tube-4x6.nom'},'6x6':{x:6,y:6,file:'tube.nom'},'8x8':{x:8,y:8,file:'tube-8x8.nom'},'10x6':{x:10,y:6,file:'tube-10x6.nom'},'12x6':{x:12,y:6,file:'tube-12x6.nom'},'14x6':{x:14,y:6,file:'tube-14x6.nom'},'6x12':{x:6,y:12,file:'tube-6x12.nom'}};let loading=false;
async function select(key){const p=PRESETS[key];if(!p||loading)return;loading=true;try{const response=await fetch('templates/'+p.file+'?mu=092wip102',{cache:'no-store'});if(!response.ok)throw Error('Verified Tube template HTTP '+response.status);const support=window.__muWIP59Support;if(!support?.loadPrimitiveBytes||!support?.use)throw Error('Verified Tube support is unavailable');support.loadPrimitiveBytes(new Uint8Array(await response.arrayBuffer()),'MeshUtilz verified '+key+' Tube.nom',true);support.use();for(const [id,value]of[['muzW84SubdivX',p.x],['muzW84SubdivY',p.y]]){const input=document.getElementById(id),out=document.getElementById(id+'Out');if(input){input.value=String(value);input.dispatchEvent(new Event('input',{bubbles:true}))}if(out)out.value=String(value)}const download=document.getElementById('muzWIP59Download');if(download)download.disabled=true;const status=document.getElementById('muzW83HairStatus');if(status){status.textContent='Verified '+key+' Tube cache loaded — press Generate';delete status.dataset.result}}catch(error){alert(error.message||error)}finally{loading=false}}

Other segment values remain experimental and are never written into export data.</p>';body.insertBefore(row,document.getElementById('muzW84Resolution')||null);row.querySelector('select').addEventListener('change',event=>select(event.target.value));}

// MeshUtilz v0.9.2-wip.103 — verified Tube-cache integrity gate
(function(){'use strict';
const PRESETS={'4x6':{x:4,y:6,file:'tube-4x6.nom',sha256:'68a1586d3456af107ffa1e8c8784c8d4fd58e88f6280dde677a3c49c4073b67c'},'6x6':{x:6,y:6,file:'tube.nom',sha256:'e7388437a924499dcb2a7ea97c7032eade41a018ada243390d7b02b3fb0059ee'},'8x8':{x:8,y:8,file:'tube-8x8.nom',sha256:'ae0c16c1676279d2f22ca8217e6bf6044dc284b8be413b7fcfd3c6309256b862'},'10x6':{x:10,y:6,file:'tube-10x6.nom',sha256:'7a4d146a153b580011e05de3ae0573509c552e09b8aec35ecb77329201b752e6'},'12x6':{x:12,y:6,file:'tube-12x6.nom',sha256:'f17e33b320d304bd7653d8c0d67e61539a963c16c031e2af2fce2b8b785a7fa7'},'14x6':{x:14,y:6,file:'tube-14x6.nom',sha256:'932bc3f0bc959f9309427abe2e247e06996f267346d063e3a98ef8f76cd7297d'},'6x12':{x:6,y:12,file:'tube-6x12.nom',sha256:'6c77543d6b597597452c022a6fa260a69aad5031fc2bcf533eaa935bdd4c3366'}};
let loading=false;
function hex(buffer){return Array.from(new Uint8Array(buffer),byte=>byte.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('')}
async function verifiedBytes(preset){const response=await fetch('templates/'+preset.file+'?mu=092wip103',{cache:'no-store'});if(!response.ok)throw Error('Verified Tube template HTTP '+response.status);const bytes=new Uint8Array(await response.arrayBuffer());if(bytes.byteLength<12||String.fromCharCode(...bytes.subarray(0,12))!=='Nomad Sculpt')throw Error('Verified Tube template is not a Nomad Sculpt file');if(!globalThis.crypto?.subtle)throw Error('Template verification requires Web Crypto');const actual=hex(await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256',bytes));if(actual!==preset.sha256)throw Error('Verified Tube template checksum mismatch');return bytes}
async function select(key){const preset=PRESETS[key];if(!preset||loading)return;loading=true;try{const support=window.__muWIP59Support;if(!support?.loadPrimitiveBytes||!support?.use)throw Error('Verified Tube support is unavailable');support.loadPrimitiveBytes(await verifiedBytes(preset),'MeshUtilz verified '+key+' Tube.nom',true);support.use();for(const [id,value]of[['muzW84SubdivX',preset.x],['muzW84SubdivY',preset.y]]){const input=document.getElementById(id),out=document.getElementById(id+'Out');if(input){input.value=String(value);input.dispatchEvent(new Event('input',{bubbles:true}))}if(out)out.value=String(value)}const download=document.getElementById('muzWIP59Download');if(download)download.disabled=true;const status=document.getElementById('muzW83HairStatus');if(status){status.textContent='Verified '+key+' Tube cache checksum passed — press Generate';delete status.dataset.result}}catch(error){alert(error.message||error)}finally{loading=false}}
function install(){const body=document.getElementById('muzW83Hair')?.querySelector('.mlr10-body');if(!body)return;document.getElementById('muzW86CachePreset')?.remove();document.getElementById('muzW102CachePreset')?.remove();if(document.getElementById('muzW103CachePreset'))return;const row=document.createElement('div');row.id='muzW103CachePreset';row.className='control';row.innerHTML='<label for="muzW103Preset">Verified Tube cache</label><select id="muzW103Preset"><option value="6x6">6 × 6 (default)</option><option value="4x6">4 × 6</option><option value="8x8">8 × 8</option><option value="10x6">10 × 6</option><option value="12x6">12 × 6</option><option value="14x6">14 × 6</option><option value="6x12">6 × 12</option></select><p class="muz83-note"><b>WIP103:</b> checks the native NOM signature and SHA-256 before loading. Other segment values are not written into export data.</p>';body.insertBefore(row,document.getElementById('muzW84Resolution')||null);row.querySelector('select').addEventListener('change',event=>select(event.target.value));}

  const file = fileInput.files?.[0];
  if (file) loadFile(file).catch((e) => { console.error(e); setStatus(`Load failed: ${e.message || e}`); });
  fileInput.value = '';
});
